# Full-App Expert-Panel Review — Desktop Web + Mobile Web + iOS (MONET, 2026-08-18)

Owner-directed top-to-bottom review of Socratic.Trade by a diverse expert panel, covering the desktop website, the phone-width website, and the native iOS app.  Every finding is grounded on current `main` (`c55c2e642` at review start) with a file:line, and every panel finding was independently re-checked by a second, skeptical agent.  Findings that were already fixed, in-flight, out of scope (the PWA), or contrary to owner rulings were meant to be refuted at that stage; the verifiers instead confirmed 307 and downgraded/qualified 29 (see §0 for the honest caveat).

Companion artifacts: the hotfix that came out of the live pass (PR #2851, live), the raw findings JSON (`review-findings.json`, session scratchpad — not committed), and Cursor's 2026-08-17 report-only audits (§10).

## 0. How To Read This

- **Method.**  Seventeen expert lanes ran in parallel over the repo and a local dev server, each returning file:line-grounded findings in a fixed schema; one skeptic per lane re-opened every citation on current code and returned CONFIRMED / PARTIAL / REFUTED / UNVERIFIABLE (with corrected severities); one agent cross-checked the result against the three prior full reviews (2026-07-20, 2026-08-04, 2026-08-06); MONET separately walked the live surfaces by hand (desktop 1280px, phone 390px, iPhone 17 Pro simulator in fixture mode) — those are the `LIVE-nn` items in §1.

- **Counts.**  336 panel findings (307 CONFIRMED, 29 PARTIAL, 0 REFUTED, 0 UNVERIFIABLE) + 20 live-pass findings + 50 verifier-added items.  By final severity: P1 40 · P2 148 · P3 148 (no panel P0; the one production-down class item was the live-pass hotfix, LIVE-09).  By kind: 231 needs-fixing (bug/defect/risk) vs 105 could-be-improved (improvement/opportunity).  By surface: Cross-surface 79, Backend 76, Desktop web 64, iOS 59, Mobile web 28, Public site 19, API 11.

- **Honest caveat on verification.**  Zero refutations across 336 items means the verifiers were lenient rather than that every claim is airtight; treat CONFIRMED as "a second reader re-opened the file and agreed", and read the PARTIAL notes as the correction of record.  Severity is the verifier's final severity.

- **Owner rulings were applied as constraints**, not re-litigated: real trading / no paternalism, guardrails advisory with off-switches, PWA out of scope, light default theme, two-space + Title Case copy rules, Central Time timestamps, sole-user (no compat shims).  Where a finding is really an owner-philosophy decision it is labelled as such (e.g. LIVE-17, tsx-11, ios-08).

- **Ids** are `lane:id` (panel) or `LIVE-nn` (live pass) so every line is traceable to its lane's raw output.  Duplicates across lanes were kept (they corroborate each other) and are noted with "same as" where obvious.

## 1. Executive Summary — What Matters Most

Ranked by impact on a real-money trader using this every day.  Each line names the finding id(s) so the detail is one search away.

1. **A production regression shipped and was fixed during the review.**  `/console/connections` (broker + LLM-key management) crashed client-side on `main` HEAD `c55c2e642` (#2848) with `process.uptime is not a function`; hotfix PR #2851 is merged and live (`01f4e73e2`).  The root cause is structural and still open: server DB modules are bundled (stubbed) into the browser via `brokers.tsx → venue-contract → source-settings → db-api-keys → db` — the Connections client bundle is 1.05 MB vs ~476 KB for every other page, and default Turbopack `npm run dev` 500s every route once that page compiles.  → LIVE-01, LIVE-09, `frontend-performance:dweb-01`.  Fix: `import "server-only"` on `src/lib/db.ts`, a client-safe home for `mergeAccountCapabilities`, and make dev use the same bundler as prod.
2. **Money path — three ways the app can do something with an order the owner did not intend.**  (a) Alpaca MCP placement falls back to REST after any ambiguous MCP failure and re-submits the same `client_order_id`; the 422 duplicate is filed as `rejected_by_broker` while the first order lives at the broker (`trading-money-path:tsx-01`).  (b) The stale-exit remediation cancel-replaces ANY working sell/cover limit or stop-limit older than 15 min with a MARKET order — including activated bracket take-profit legs, bracket stop-limit legs and owner-placed GTC sells (`tsx-02`).  (c) Manually cancelling an app-managed protective stop is silently undone next tick (`tsx-08`); any broker 4xx including 429/408 is filed as a terminal broker rejection (`tsx-04`); approval-time re-anchoring moves EXIT limits with the market (`tsx-09`).
3. **iOS tells the owner an approval succeeded when it did not.**  `proposal.approve` returns `blocked / busy / not_placed / error / rejected_by_broker` as a resolved result, `runCommand` stamps it `succeeded`, and the phone shows a green "Approved — waiting for desk refresh" (`api-contract-web-ios:api-02`, `trading-money-path:tsx-03`).  Related: iOS Guardrails stop-loss rows always show "—" because `FullPolicy` decodes `stopLossPct` at the top level while the server nests it under `riskRules` (`api-01`); the iOS scan timeout (25s) is below the new server budget (35s) so slow scans read as "Check your connection" (`api-03`).
4. **P&L is quietly wrong in four independent ways.**  The fill ledger keeps the OLDEST 500 fills per source (`ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT 500`, sixteen callers) so every P&L / open-lot / tax / model-stats figure freezes once an account passes 500 fills (`performance-accounting:perf-01`, `backend-reliability:berel-02`, prior 2026-07-03 #39); Home Day P&L is zeroed on ordinary trading days because flow inference runs with no fills and reads market P&L as a deposit (`perf-02`); short/cover cash sign is inverted in flow inference (`perf-03`, prior 2026-08-06 C1 root cause); tax "Short-term realized" excludes every covered short (`perf-07`); win rate counts partial closes as round trips (`perf-11`); "Versus the market" TWR silently covers only ~7 trading days (`perf-04`).
5. **The Green-team 400s of the last two days have a concrete cause.**  The Bull strict JSON schema lists `exitPlan` in `properties` but not `required`; OpenAI-family strict mode rejects that with HTTP 400 — misdiagnosed as "terra unservable" (`llm-agent-architecture:llm-01`).  Also: the Red Team reviewer resolves the ACTIVE console account, not the account under review (`llm-02`); delta-only macro pruning strips unchanged FRED fields the prompt tells the model to use (`llm-03`); the daily learning review auto-applies `strategy-directive` rows into the strategy prompt as trusted owner text (`llm-04`); "Retry Red Team" stamps approve-at-half without applying the haircut (`llm-06`); the hard-coded dead-model list drops `claude-fable-5`/`kimi-latest` from rotation (`llm-10`).
6. **Market-data freshness is fabricated in several places.**  Weekend TTL extension fires during Friday's live session so screener/OHLC/enrichment caches freeze until Monday (`market-data-integrity:mdi-01`); daily bars are served "fresh" for 3 calendar days and today's partial bar is persisted as an EOD close (`mdi-02`); the cascade stamps cache-served values with a "now" `asOf`, so 6-hour-old Nasdaq quote/volume wins recency arbitration (`mdi-03`); Yahoo/ROIC/CBOE fallbacks stamp `asOf = now`, defeating the stale-quote gate that converts market orders to limit backups (`mdi-04`); Alpaca "price" is the ASK (`mdi-05`).
7. **Backend: the event-loop pin has a root cause and the broker has no timeout.**  FTS5 idempotency DELETE/COUNT use equality on non-rowid columns = a full-table scan per chunk on a 4.2 GB DB (`backend-reliability:berel-01`); filing-body ingest, `persistLocalComplete` and the summarizer still mirror whole documents into FTS synchronously (`berel-04`); Alpaca SDK/Tradier calls have no HTTP timeout and the stop-monitor lanes only release when the hung promise settles (`berel-03`); the scheduler tick has no re-entrancy guard (`berel-08`); `getEquityOrders` walks the entire order history (up to 50×500) on every snapshot and 20 other call sites (`dweb-02`/`tsx-05`/`berel-09`).
8. **Security: one config slip makes anonymous = owner.**  Production has no `AUTH_SECRET` boot guard; if the secret is missing every anonymous request becomes the primary user (`security-auth:sec-01`, see also LIVE-20 hardcoded owner email as the fallback identity).  Sessions cannot be revoked (30-day stateless JWT, `sec-04`); origin is directly reachable at `167.233.254.55:443` bypassing Cloudflare (`sec-02`); `next-auth 5.0.0-beta.31` carries three published advisories (`sec-03`); mobile auth exchange buffers unbounded bodies with no rate limit (`sec-06`); the mobile web-auth handoff mints a session code for any authenticated GET with an arbitrary `code_challenge` (`sec-05`).
9. **iOS: sign-out leaves the previous account's snapshot in UserDefaults** so a cold relaunch boots stale data and mislabels an explicit sign-out as "session expired" (`ios-engineering:ios-01`); guardrail/account edits in the Settings sheet fail silently because `store.error` is never rendered there (`ios-hig-ux:ios-01`); a 3-minute staleness gate disables Approve/Run after any quiet 3 minutes with no polling fallback (`ios-engineering:ios-03`); one malformed list element blanks the whole app (`api-07`); Swift tests never run in CI or before TestFlight and the Swift build is advisory, not a merge gate (`qa-01`, `qa-02`); `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` is absent (`ios-engineering:ios-10`); Coach renders markdown as raw text (`ios-hig-ux:ios-02`); "1 Proposals for Review" (LIVE-15); money formatting drops cents (LIVE-14).
10. **Ops: nothing pages the owner when the trader stops trading.**  `schedulerStale`, `tradingLivenessDegraded` and OpenRouter low-balance are computed but never pushed (`ops-observability-admin:sre-02`); there is no at-a-glance "is the trader alive" panel on web/admin/iOS (`sre-01`); litestream kill-switch markers are invisible to `/api/health` and the Backups page (`sre-03`, prior 2026-08-06 A2); three backup layers, zero restore drills (`sre-14`); a broker-held protective stop declined/failed is audit-only (`sre-12`); an overdue next run is never flagged (`sre-11`).
11. **Web console correctness at the top of the funnel.**  Home proposal rows carry a dead/wrong Approve button that POSTs a synthetic id (404) and wrong status tones (`desktop-web-ux:dweb-01`); a bare `r` key fires a strategy run and two mounted RunOnce buttons double-POST (`dweb-02`); "Recent decisions" rows are click-only divs — the Approve flow is unreachable by keyboard (`web-accessibility:a11y-01`); tone-colored chips and the Stop/Start button fail WCAG AA in the default light theme (`a11y-02`, prior #2561); the phone chrome bar squeezes the account scope to ~0–15px since the label became "Stop Agent" (`mobile-web-ux:mweb-01`, LIVE-11); "System" theme never survives reload and ⌘K theme toggle does nothing visible (`mweb-03`, `mweb-04`).
12. **QA gates have holes that let all of the above ship.**  Docs-only CI fast path skips the build for build-time-imported `docs/benchmarks/*.json` (`qa-test-strategy:qa-03`); no API→iOS snapshot contract test (`qa-04`); no global network guard in vitest, so the suite still reaches SEC/Yahoo/SiliconFlow (`qa-05`); 63/122 routes untested including single-proposal approve (`qa-07`); browser coverage is one 18-line desktop smoke, never on PRs, no phone width (`qa-06`); `land.sh` skips lint and never compiles Swift (`qa-11`).
13. **Copy is inconsistent in ways that misinform.**  Macro page says below-median buys are "hard-vetoed" in crisis regimes; the code is advisory tag-not-drop (`ux-copy-editor:copy-03`); iOS sends the owner to a "Strategy page" that does not exist in the app (`copy-04`); "Approvals" vs "Proposals", three names for one run state, four names for the stop control (`copy-05`, `copy-07`); ALL-CAPS "(NOT Real Money)" labels (`copy-11`); a "Mock (offline)" model offered first in the Coach picker (`copy-16`); the two-space rule is unenforced across ~600 strings and double ASCII spaces collapse at render (`copy-01`, `copy-02`); Guardrails header says "hard execution constraints" (LIVE-04).
14. **Public site over-promises and under-discloses.**  Explainer copy promises delegation controls the product does not have (`public-site-growth:dpub-03`); `/framework` states an invariant that is not true (`dpub-04`); Terms were not updated when market-data pooling became a condition of use (`dpub-05`); every Auth.js error lands on `/access-denied` with no way forward (`dpub-02`); session-expired redirect drops the deep-link destination (`dpub-01`).
15. **Still open from prior reviews** (§6): Run once not stamped with account alias + LIVE|PAPER (2026-08-04 R1), iOS one-tap Stop vs web confirm parity (R4), duplicate stale-quote annotations (2026-08-06 C6), single-account Alpaca trade-updates stream (2026-07-20 #43), Coach drafts cannot express short/cover (#45), Anthropic prompt caching dead under OpenRouter (#15).

## 2. Live-Pass Findings (MONET, by hand)

Captured on a fresh `origin/main` worktree: `next dev` (Turbopack, then `--webpack`) at desktop 1280×720 and phone 390×844, plus the iOS app on a dedicated iPhone 17 Pro simulator in `-ASCScreenshots` fixture mode.  These are additive to the panel and are cited from §1.

### LIVE-01 (P1, cross-surface / dev workflow) — `npm run dev` (Turbopack) 500s the whole app after `/console/connections`
- Evidence: fresh worktree at origin/main c55c2e642, `npm run dev -- -p 3005` (Turbopack default in Next 16). `/console` 200 until `/console/connections` compiled → "Module not found: Can't resolve 'fs'" via `./node_modules/better-sqlite3/lib/database.js` from Client Component chain: `app/console/settings/brokers.tsx` ("use client") → `src/lib/venue-contract.ts:22 import { resolveSourceBool } from "./source-settings"` → `src/lib/source-settings.ts:15-16 import { LOCAL_USER } from "./db-api-keys"; import ... from "./db-settings"` → `src/lib/db.ts` → better-sqlite3. After that error every route (`/login`, `/welcome`, `/console/*`, `/admin/*`) returned 500 until restart (verified with curl over all 34 static routes).
- Prod build passes only because `package.json` `"build": "next build --webpack"` and `next.config.mjs:39-66` alias `better-sqlite3`/`fs`/`dns`/... to `false` for client bundles — i.e. server DB code is being bundled (stubbed) into the browser bundle rather than excluded. With `npx next dev --webpack -p 3005` the page is 200 (verified).
- Why: AGENTS.md tells every agent to verify with local `npm run dev`; today that path is broken on a key page and takes the whole app down; also masks a real client/server boundary leak (module bloat + accidental server code on the client).
- Recommendation: (1) add `import "server-only"` at top of `src/lib/db.ts` (and the `db-*.ts` modules) so any client import fails the build loudly; (2) move the pure `mergeAccountCapabilities` (and any other pure venue helpers) out of `src/lib/venue-contract.ts` into a client-safe module (or make `source-settings` not import `db-api-keys` for `LOCAL_USER`); (3) either mirror the webpack aliases into `turbopack.resolveAlias` in `next.config.mjs` or change the `dev` script to `next dev --webpack` so dev == prod bundler; (4) one-line AGENTS.md note.

### LIVE-02 (P2, desktop web) — Bare default 404 page inside the console shell
- `/console/proposals` (a natural guess for the "Proposals" nav label, which actually links to `/console/approvals`) renders Next's stock "404 | This page could not be found." on a gradient — no nav, no wordmark, no link back. No `app/not-found.tsx` / `app/console/not-found.tsx` (verify).
- Rec: add `app/not-found.tsx` (and console-scoped one) with the console chrome + "Back to Home".  Consider `redirect('/console/approvals')` from `/console/proposals` since nav label and iOS both say "Proposals".

### LIVE-03 (P2, copy) — Button/link casing inconsistent in the console top bar + checklist
- Top bar: "Run once" (sentence case) next to "Start Agent" (Title Case); checklist links "Open Connections", "Choose account", "Add API key"; Scan tabs "Market scan" / "Smart money". FLEET-UI-COPY says buttons/nav/tabs Title Case.

### LIVE-04 (P2, copy/philosophy) — Guardrails header says "hard execution constraints"; Autonomy panel says it "does not duplicate those buttons" and then shows a Start Agent button
- `/console/guardrails` header: "authority, caps, and hard execution constraints" vs owner ruling that guardrails are advisory/adjustable.  Autonomy card copy: "Run once and Start/Stop live in the top bar only (one control each — this panel explains state and blockers, it does not duplicate those buttons)" immediately followed by a green "Start Agent" button in the same card.

### LIVE-05 (P2, desktop web) — Proposals page: "The server refuses approving or rejecting proposals while stopped"
- Rejecting a proposal is data (the page itself says "Rejections are data, not failures"), yet the stopped state blocks both approve AND reject.  At minimum reject/annotate should work while Stopped; approve-while-stopped could queue.  Check API: which route enforces this and whether iOS mirrors it.

These selected models do not expose a provider-specific reasoning or thinking control here." even when no model is selected; fallback placeholders "e.g. gpt-5.4-mini, claude-sonnet-5" (stale/odd example ids).

### LIVE-09 (P1 → HOTFIXED, PR #2851) — Production `/console/connections` "Dashboard error: process.uptime is not a function"
- Prod `c55c2e642` (#2848, live 00:51Z). `src/lib/db-execution.ts:329` module-scope `process.uptime()`; module reachable from browser bundle via db barrel; Next browser `process` shim has no `uptime`. Reproduced on `next dev --webpack` (same bundler as prod). Fix = lazy guarded accessor + regression test proven to fail on old code. Root cause (server modules in client bundle) remains LIVE-01.
