Any unauthenticated local dev build presents as "Signed in as mail@jays.services" (primary + admin). Sole-user today so low severity, but the fallback should be a neutral placeholder (or required env) so a misconfigured deploy cannot silently make an anonymous session the primary user — verify how `authConfigured=false` + PRIMARY_EMAIL interact in production.

## 3. P1 Findings (40) — Wrong Information Or Broken Core Flow

### Needs Fixing (40)

#### [backend-reliability:berel-01] FTS5 idempotency DELETE/COUNT use equality on non-rowid columns = full-table scan per chunk; this, not tokenization, is the ~165-300ms/row that pins the loop
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** rag/fts-mirror · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/db-learning.ts:1671`
- **Evidence** DELETE FROM document_chunks_fts WHERE content_hash = ? AND symbol = ? AND source = ? AND accession = ? (db.ts:2148 fts5(content_hash, symbol, source, accession, text) — no UNINDEXED, no MATCH)
- **Why it matters** FTS5 xBestIndex only indexes MATCH and rowid constraints; a WHERE on ordinary columns is a residual filter evaluated over a full scan of the %_content shadow table. Every mirrored chunk therefore costs one full scan of the entire FTS corpus (4.2 GB DB), and countDocumentChunkFts (:1706), hasLocalFilingCopy/countLocalFtsRows (persist-local-complete.ts:67-72,108-113 with GLOB) do the same. The per-row cost grows with corpus size — matches the receipts (165 ms/row on 08-13, ~300 ms/row on 08-14, 6-13 s ftsMirrorSlice for six 1-row groups on 08-18) and explains why every yield/slice bound has only stretched the pin instead of removing it. Also all four metadata columns are tokenized into the FT…
- **Recommendation** Give each occurrence an indexable identity: either (a) a plain side table document_chunk_fts_keys(symbol,source,accession,content_hash,fts_rowid) with a UNIQUE index, DELETE FROM document_chunks_fts WHERE rowid = ?, and COUNT from the side table; or (b) rewrite the identity predicates as column-filter MATCH ('symbol:"X" AND source:"Y" AND accession:"Z" AND content_hash:"H"'), which uses the FTS index. Declare content_hash/source/accession UNINDEXED if you take (a). Wrap del.run/ins.run in timeSync to prove the split before/after. One-time rebuild migration for the corpus.

#### [backend-reliability:berel-03] Broker calls have no HTTP timeout (Alpaca SDK axios default 0; Tradier fetch has no signal) and the scheduler's stop-monitor / stale-exit lanes are guarded by in-flight sets that only release when the hung promise settles
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** scheduler/broker · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/scheduler.ts:914`
- **Evidence** if (protectiveState && !stopMonitorInFlight.has(key)) { stopMonitorInFlight.add(key); void journalLane("synthetic-stop-monitor", ..., () => runSyntheticStopMonitor(...)) ... .finally(() => stopMonitorInFlight.delete(key));
- **Why it matters** safety-maintenance.ts:74-77 wraps the same monitor in withDeadline(15s) but the scheduler lane (the one that actually runs every tick) does not; tradier.ts:339 `await fetch(url, { method, headers, body })` and @alpacahq/alpaca-trade-api (dist/api.js `return axios(req)`, no timeout; alpaca.ts:256-287 trackHealth adds retries, not deadlines) can hang on a half-open socket until TCP retransmit gives up (~15 min on Linux). During that window the account's synthetic-stop monitor and stale-exit replacement are silently skipped every tick, and the tick's serial `await checkBrokerHealth` (:953) blocks every later account in the loop. The 08-18 first-call retry only covers getAccount/getPortfolio/ge…
- **Recommendation** Give every gateway method a hard deadline at the adapter boundary: `AbortSignal.timeout(BROKER_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS)` in TradierClient.request and PublicBroker fetches; for Alpaca wrap each SDK call in withDeadline (or set axios defaults timeout on the SDK's axios instance) so a hung read rejects in ~20 s. In scheduler.ts wrap the stop-monitor, stale-limit-scan, pending-fill-reconcile and checkBrokerHealth lanes with withDeadline like safety-maintenance does, and clear the in-flight key on deadline so the next tick can retry.

#### [backend-reliability:berel-04] filing-body-ingest, persistLocalComplete and document-summarizer still mirror whole documents into FTS synchronously with no yield and no in-flight-strategy gate — the same pin class the SEC worker was bounded against
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** rag/ingest · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/web-sources/sec-filings.ts:736`
- **Evidence** runWithActiveVectorCommitProof(result.managedCommitProof, () => timeSync("filingFtsMirror", ..., () => { for (const chunk of localChunks) { insertDocumentChunkFts(...) } insertIngestedAccession(...)
- **Why it matters** refreshFilingBodies runs daily from scheduler.ts:602 (SEC_FILING_RAG_MAX_PER_RUN default 25) and is not gated by hasInFlightStrategyWork; its FTS mirror is a single synchronous transaction over every chunk of a 10-K (each insertDocumentChunkFts doing the full-scan DELETE from berel-01), so a 900-chunk filing can pin the serving loop for minutes exactly like the 08-13/08-14 incidents. persist-local-complete.ts:48-53 and rag/document-summarizer.ts:521 have the same shape (sync loop, per-row delete+insert, no yieldEventLoop). Only sec-ingest-worker.ts:463-476 got the slice/yield/heartbeat treatment.
- **Recommendation** Route every FTS mirror through insertDocumentChunkFtsBatch (adaptive groups + yields) and the same per-tick slice/resume policy, gate them on hasInFlightStrategyWork like ROIC/SEC worker, and land berel-01 so each row is O(1). Until then, either disable the FTS half of filing-body-ingest or run it only outside market hours.

#### [llm-agent-architecture:llm-01] Bull strict JSON schema lists `exitPlan` in properties but not in `required` — OpenAI-family Green seats 400 since 2026-08-16 (misdiagnosed as 'terra unservable')
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** strategy structured output / model failover · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/strategy.ts:5847`
- **Evidence** exitPlan: { type: ["string", "null"], description: "Staged exit plan..." } … required: [...BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS] (BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS at :6630 has 16 keys, no exitPlan)
- **Why it matters** buildLlmRequestBody sends `json_schema … strict: true` (llm-call.ts:515/529). OpenAI strict mode rejects any schema whose `required` omits a property with HTTP 400 invalid_json_schema; through OpenRouter that surfaces as the fast 'HTTP 400 Provider returned error (881ms)' the 2026-08-18 rollout notes chased for gpt-5.6-terra. #2740 (2026-08-16) added exitPlan to properties only. Every gpt-* rotation seat (terra/sol/luna/5.4-mini/nano/4o) and any gpt primary hits this; #2831 then hardcoded terra as 'unservable' instead of fixing the schema, so runs fail over to the cheapest preferred seats. filterRepairedProposals' projection (:6787) also drops exitPlan on the repair path.
- **Recommendation** Add "exitPlan" to BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS (schema + projection use it) and add a unit test that, for every strict schema (Bull items, autonomyOverride, stopPlan, Red verdict, revalidation, tuning), asserts Object.keys(properties) ⊆ required. Re-verify a gpt-* Green run, then remove UNSERVABLE_OPENROUTER_FIRST_PICKS/terra demotion if it was only this. Grep llm_call_latency for status 400 by model since 2026-08-16 to confirm blast radius.

#### [llm-agent-architecture:llm-02] Red Team reviewer resolves the ACTIVE console account, not the account under review (venue/short capability, execution mode, strategy prompt)
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** Red Team adversary flow / multi-account correctness · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/red-team.ts:192`
- **Evidence** const activeAccount = getActiveConnectedAccount(userId); const venue = deriveVenueContract(policy, activeAccount); … const basePrompt = getStrategyPrompt(userId);
- **Why it matters** The strategy run passes an account-scoped runPolicy (policy.connectedAccountId) and itself uses getConnectedAccount(connectedAccountId) (strategy.ts:494), but debateProposal re-derives venue/executionState/strategy prompt from whichever account has is_active=1 (the console switcher). With Paper + Roth scheduled concurrently, a Paper short can be refused with 'This <Roth> account cannot short' (:194-201) without any reviewer call, and the reviewer reads the other account's strategy prompt (getStrategyPrompt falls back to the active account, db-profiles.ts:323-325). Cross-account context bleed in a money-path review.
- **Recommendation** Resolve the account from policy.connectedAccountId (getConnectedAccount(id, userId)), fall back to active only when absent; call getStrategyPrompt(userId, policy.connectedAccountId); add a test with two connected accounts where the non-active one is reviewed.

#### [llm-agent-architecture:llm-03] Delta-only macro pruning strips unchanged FRED fields from every run after the first — the stateless model never sees values the prompt tells it to use
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Backend · **Area** prompt assembly / context quality · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/strategy.ts:4960`
- **Evidence** const { macro: macroForPrompt, omitted: macroOmitted } = pruneMacro(macro, previousMacro); setInternalSetting(macroCacheKey, macro); … ? { ...macroForPrompt, unchangedSinceLastRun: macroOmitted }
- **Why it matters** pruneMacro (macro.ts:594-616) keeps only vix/fedFundsRate/dgs10Treasury/asOf plus fields that changed since `last_macro_sent:${userId}`; every other field (hyCreditSpread, dgs2, CPI, unemployment, usdIndex, wtiOil, corePCE…) is replaced by a bare list of names. LLM calls are stateless and the seat rotates models per run, so 'unchanged since last run' carries no information — yet buildBullSystem (strategy-prompts.ts:185) instructs the model to read hyCreditSpread and the curve together for recession risk. FRED series change daily at most while runs happen many times a day, so almost every run is starved. The key is user-scoped (shared across accounts) and the marker is written before the LLM…
- **Recommendation** Send the full macro block every run (it is ~30 short fields; token savings are negligible next to the 48k-char evidence budget) — delete pruneMacro's delta path or key it by (userId, accountId, model) and include values. Add a prompt-wiring test asserting hyCreditSpread/dgs2Treasury appear on a second consecutive run.

#### [llm-agent-architecture:llm-04] Daily LLM learning review auto-approves strategy-directive pending rows into the strategy prompt as TRUSTED owner text (contract says human approval required)
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** learning loop / prompt trust boundary · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/learning-review.ts:660`
- **Evidence** applyApprovedPending(pending, nowIso); action = setPendingLearnedContextStatus(verdict.id, userId, "approved") ? "approved" : null;
- **Why it matters** listPendingLearnedContext(userId, 'pending') (:479) includes riskTier 'strategy-directive' rows; a 'keep' verdict in the default learningReviewMode 'decide' (defaults.ts:90) calls applyApprovedPending, which appends an AI-LEARNED block to the strategy prompt (learned-context/store.ts:399-404). coach-learning.ts:8-9 promises 'human approval required before they touch the strategy prompt', and prompt-safety treats the whole strategy prompt as the ONLY trusted source (prompt-safety.ts:225; strategy.ts:4934) — so an LLM verdict promotes chat-derived text into the one region the data-not-command boundary does not cover, for both Green and Red (red-team.ts:257 sends it raw).
- **Recommendation** Exclude strategy-directive rows from auto-apply (route them to 'defer'/human) or, if the owner wants LLM approval, contain AI-LEARNED blocks as 'learned' (untrusted) rather than owner_strategy. Add a test that a 'keep' verdict on a strategy-directive row does not call setStrategyPrompt.

#### [market-data-integrity:mdi-01] Weekend TTL extension fires during Friday's live session: screener/OHLC/enrichment caches freeze until Monday
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** market-data caching / market hours · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/market-hours.ts:288`
- **Evidence** isWeekendOrHolidayClosureAhead: `if (!isEtCalendarTradingDay(today)) return true; return !isEtCalendarTradingDay(addEtCalendarDays(today, 1));` -> true all day Friday; expiresAt = Monday 9:30
- **Why it matters** Verified with tsx: a 5-minute screener write at Fri 10:00 ET expires 2026-08-24T13:30Z (Monday open). Same on the day before any holiday. So every Friday strategy run and Scan refresh reuses the FIRST screener rows of the day (market.ts:1342), OHLC bars (history.ts:243/321), 6h enrichment (data-providers.ts:2083/3881/4098) and macro caches. Ranking, momentum (intradayChangePct), breadth and universe-floor decisions run on frozen data with a fresh generatedAt; only top-candidate prices are rescued by the broker cascade.
- **Recommendation** Only extend when no session remains between now and the naive expiry: gate on `nowMs >= etWallClockToUtcMs(today,16,0)` (or naiveExpiry past today's close) before walking to next open; add tests for Fri 10:00 ET / Wed-before-Thanksgiving 10:00 ET expecting naive TTL.

#### [market-data-integrity:mdi-04] asOf fabricated from the wall clock (Yahoo quote-only fallback, ROIC profile price, CBOE, grouped VWAP) defeats the quote-staleness gate
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** quote freshness / staleness gate · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/market.ts:1520`
- **Evidence** toQuoteOnlyMarketQuote: `provider: "yahoo-finance", asOf: new Date().toISOString()` while `quote.asOf` (Yahoo regularMarketTime) is ignored; quotes-cascade.ts:397 ROIC `asOf: new Date().toISOString()`
- **Why it matters** policy.ts:412-413 ages `quote.asOf`; a fabricated 'now' makes a delayed/EOD print look 0s old so the market order is not converted to the limit backup, and quotes-cascade `updateBestQuote` (lines 181-186) prefers the fabricated newer stamp over a real broker timestamp. cboe.ts:47 and mergeGroupedBarData (market.ts:1243, GroupedDailyBar has no `date`) do the same for signals shown with an as-of.
- **Recommendation** Propagate provider timestamps (Yahoo regularMarketTime already parsed at yahoo-finance.ts:76-77/185-186; CBOE last_trade_time; grouped bar date); when the provider gives none leave asOf undefined so isQuoteFresh/quoteAgeSecForStalenessGate treat it as stale.

#### [ops-observability-admin:sre-02] schedulerStale, tradingLivenessDegraded and OpenRouter low-balance never trigger an in-app push; only external keyword monitors can page
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** alerting / notifications · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED · **Prior** in-flight PR #2816 (adds JSON flags + UptimeRobot runbook only, no in-app push)
- **Where** `app/api/health/route.ts:128`
- **Evidence** const liveness = getTradingLivenessSummary(); ... if (liveness.degraded) checks.tradingLivenessDegraded = true; // no sendNotification anywhere for this flag
- **Why it matters** Money-critical degradations are computed but only serialized: (a) schedulerStale (route.ts:82-88) has no notify path (Sentry Crons check-in is opt-in on SENTRY_CRONS_ENABLED=1, docs/ops-observability-security.md); (b) tradingLivenessDegraded is consumed only by /api/health and ops-snapshot.ts (rg confirms no other consumer); (c) OpenRouter prepaid balance (route.ts:300-326, src/lib/openrouter-credits.ts) — the single point of failure for every LLM call and all embedding — has zero in-app notification and is not shown anywhere on web/iOS (rg 'remainingUsd|openrouterCredits' in app/, ios/ returns nothing). The owner depends entirely on UptimeRobot→Pushover being configured for exact JSON subs…
- **Recommendation** In the scheduler tick (after the heartbeat write) evaluate the same three signals and fire deduped notifications: `risk_advisory`/new `engine_stalled` when a leader tick sees no completed run for N×cadence during market hours or consecutiveFailedRuns ≥ threshold, and `budget_alert` when credits.ok flips false — with a 'recovered' follow-up. Lower TRADING_LIVENESS_STALE_MINUTES default to max(60, 3×runCadenceMinutes). Surface OpenRouter remaining USD on /console/usage and /admin (admin-gated).

#### [qa-test-strategy:qa-03] Docs-only CI fast path skips the build for build-time-imported docs/benchmarks JSON
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** CI classify · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `.github/workflows/ci.yml:141`
- **Evidence** non_docs="$(printf '%s\n' "$changed_files" | grep -Ev '(^|/)[^/]+\.md$|^docs/' || true)" — but app/api/llm-usage/model-stats/route.ts:9 `import benchmarkJson from "../../../../docs/benchmarks/2026-07-08-llm-model-benchmark.json"`
- **Why it matters** A PR that edits only docs/benchmarks/*.json (or any non-.md under docs/) is classified docs-only, `verify` PASSes without lint/tsc/test/build, auto-merge lands it, and main auto-deploys — a malformed or reshaped JSON breaks the production build or silently changes model-stats numbers shown on /console/usage. Same regex in e2e.yml:77.
- **Recommendation** Tighten both regexes to only .md files: `grep -Ev '\.md$'` (drop the `^docs/` clause), or explicitly exclude `^docs/benchmarks/`. Add a comment pointing at the runtime import so nobody re-widens it.

#### [qa-test-strategy:qa-05] No global network guard in vitest; suite still reaches SEC/Yahoo/SiliconFlow
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Backend · **Area** Test determinism · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `vitest.config.ts:31`
- **Evidence** no `setupFiles` in vitest.config.ts; STATUS.md:59 “Full `npm test` still hits leftover SiliconFlow + SEC/Yahoo 404s (untouched)”; 2026-07-05 rollout: “Other test files may still make live provider calls”
- **Why it matters** Any test that reaches src/lib/market.ts, data-providers.ts, or vector-db without a per-file stub makes real HTTP with 6–8s abort timeouts and 429 backoff, so CI is nondeterministic under provider rate limits and each new file can silently regress. This is also the stated reason maxWorkers was pinned to 1.
- **Recommendation** Add test/setup-network-guard.ts registered via `setupFiles` that replaces globalThis.fetch with a stub that throws `Unmocked network call to <host>` for any host other than 127.0.0.1/localhost unless a file opts in with `process.env.ALLOW_TEST_NETWORK=1`; run the suite once, fix the files that fail, then keep the guard on.

#### [security-auth:sec-01] Production boots with no AUTH_SECRET guard: a missing secret turns every anonymous request into the owner
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Backend · **Area** auth / middleware fail-closed · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `middleware.ts:385`
- **Evidence** if (!trustedEmail && !isAuthConfigured()) { trustedEmail = PRIMARY_EMAIL; identitySource = ...localFallback; } (isAuthConfigured = !!AUTH_SECRET || CF flag, L165-167)
- **Why it matters** The only thing separating production from the dev fallback is the presence of the AUTH_SECRET env var. instrumentation.ts:47-48 refuses to boot without ENCRYPTION_KEY but has no equivalent for AUTH_SECRET, and request-user.ts:31 maps a header-less request to DEV_USER_ID="local", which is literally the primary owner's account id (identity.ts:56). Infisical keys have been deleted by agents more than once (AGENTS.md); one deleted/renamed AUTH_SECRET and the live real-money console is fully open to the internet as the owner, with no error anywhere. Exploit: attacker GETs /console or POSTs /api/proposals/{id}/approve with no cookie and is the owner.
- **Recommendation** Mirror the ENCRYPTION_KEY guard: in instrumentation.ts throw when NODE_ENV=production (or DB_BOOTSTRAP=live) and neither AUTH_SECRET nor an armed CF Access config is present. In middleware.ts, gate the local fallback additionally on a runtime production marker (DB_BOOTSTRAP !== "live") so the edge path fails closed even if the boot assert is bypassed. Optionally make resolveRequestUser deny (not default to "local") when the middleware email header is absent in production. Add a middleware-auth.test.ts case: AUTH_SECRET unset + DB_BOOTSTRAP=live -> 401.

#### [trading-money-path:tsx-01] Alpaca MCP placement falls back to REST after any MCP error and re-submits the same client_order_id; the duplicate 422 is then filed as rejected_by_broker while the first order lives at the broker
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** order placement / idempotency · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/alpaca.ts:346`
- **Evidence** catch (e) { console.warn(`Alpaca MCP tool call "${toolName}" failed, falling back to REST:`, e); return fallbackFn(); } (used by placeEquityOrder at :772; strategy-execution.ts:1279 treats /HTTP 4\d\d/ as terminal)
- **Why it matters** callMcp() falls back on timeout (AbortSignal 8s), MCP 5xx, or JSON parse error even when the MCP server already forwarded the POST to Alpaca. The REST retry reuses input.refId as client_order_id, Alpaca answers 422 'client_order_id must be unique', and both the approval and autonomous lanes short-circuit that 4xx to rejected_by_broker WITHOUT calling reconcilePlacementError — so a real, possibly filled order is recorded as rejected, no fill receipt, no stop plan, no daily-notional accounting. Latent today (both prod accounts are Alpaca REST per STATUS.md) but alpaca-mcp is a supported activeBroker.
- **Recommendation** For place_* tools, do NOT fall back to REST on ambiguous failures (timeout/5xx/undecodable): throw a non-4xx 'uncertain' error so reconcilePlacementError runs the refId lookup. Also treat an Alpaca 422 whose body mentions client_order_id uniqueness as 'placed-elsewhere' and route it to reconcilePlacementError instead of the 4xx terminal branch (both strategy-execution.ts:1279 and strategy.ts:3979).

#### [trading-money-path:tsx-02] Auto stale-exit remediation cancel-replaces ANY working sell/cover limit or stop_limit older than 15 min with a MARKET order — including activated bracket take-profit legs, bracket stop_limit legs and owner-placed GTC sells
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Backend · **Area** order lifecycle / stale-limit remediation · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/order-replacement.ts:755`
- **Evidence** for (const item of stale) { const side = ...; if (side !== "sell" && side !== "cover") continue; if (... === "held") continue; ... INSERT INTO order_replacements ... 'cancel_requested' (stale = listStaleLimitOrders: LIMIT_ORDER_TYPES = limit|stop_limit)
- **Why it matters** stale-limit-orders.ts:33-49 lists every working limit/stop_limit sell older than staleLimitOrderMinutes (default 15) measured from updatedAt; the only exclusion is Alpaca 'held'. An Alpaca bracket TP leg flips held->new when the entry fills and is exactly this shape 15 min later; the test at test/stale-limit-orders.test.ts:76 even asserts it is listed. autoRemediateStaleExits defaults true (defaults.ts:160); on broker/paper, or on live with requireTypedConfirmation=false, the pump cancels the TP leg (Alpaca cascades the OCO stop leg) and places a market sell of the full remaining qty — liquidating a position 15 min after entry for no thesis reason. Owner GTC take-profit trims and bracket st…
- **Recommendation** Scope remediation to app-originated protective exits only: match clientOrderId prefix sstop-/proposal refIds whose proposal.tradeThesisTag is Risk-Exit/Synthetic Stop, and exclude any order with orderClass bracket/oto/oco/legs and any order not created by the app. Add a regression test that an activated bracket TP leg and a manual GTC sell are never enqueued.

#### [api-contract-web-ios:api-01] iOS Guardrails stop-loss rows always show "—": FullPolicy reads stopLossPct at top level, server nests it under riskRules
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** iOS <-> GET /api/policy contract · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/DeskModels.swift:323`
- **Evidence** stopLossPct = try values.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .stopLossPct) // vs src/lib/types.ts:1188 `riskRules: RiskRules;` and types.ts:713-731 where stopLossPct/trailingStopPct/shortStopLossPct live inside RiskRules
- **Why it matters** GET /api/policy returns getPolicy(userId) verbatim (app/api/policy/route.ts:42), and TradingPolicy has no top-level stop fields — they are riskRules.stopLossPct etc. (DEFAULT_RISK_RULES stopLossPct: 8, defaults.ts:37). The Swift FullPolicy CodingKeys (DeskModels.swift:329-336) never touch riskRules, so GuardrailsView.swift:100-102 "Stop Loss / Trailing Stop / Short Stop" render "—" for every account since #2704 (2026-08-15). A trader on iPhone concludes no protective stop is configured when an 8% stop is live.
- **Recommendation** Add a nested `riskRules` Decodable (stopLossPct, trailingStopPct, shortStopLossPct, takeProfitPct, maxDrawdownPct) to FullPolicy and read those in GuardrailsView; drop the phantom top-level keys. Add the fixture-backed decode test described in api-06 so the next rename is caught.

#### [api-contract-web-ios:api-02] proposal.approve command reports "succeeded" (green check on iOS) when the broker order was blocked / not placed / errored
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** mobile command result contract (money path) · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/mobile-api.ts:965`
- **Evidence** case "proposal.approve": return executeProposal(String(payload.proposalId), command.userId, {...}) // executeProposal returns {status:"blocked"|"error"|"not_placed"|"busy"|"proposed", reasons} WITHOUT throwing (strategy-execution.ts:240-246, 298, 498, 1136, 1394, 1403)
- **Why it matters** runCommand only throws for a handful of pre-checks; every non-placement outcome comes back as a resolved result, so finishCommand stamps status='succeeded' with result.status='blocked'. iOS MobileCommand (MobileModels.swift:507-524) does not decode `result`, so MobileStore.proposalActionFeedback (MobileStore.swift:210-212) returns .succeeded and ProposalsView.swift:480-481 paints a green checkmark "Approved — waiting for desk refresh." on a card that stays pending (or on a broker-rejected order). Only web /api/proposals/:id/approve surfaces reasons. Same pattern for strategy.run_once: only status==='failed' throws (mobile-api.ts:954); skipped_market_closed/skipped_budget arrive as 'succeede…
- **Recommendation** In runCommand treat approve results whose status is not placed/filled as failures (throw new Error(reasons.join(' ')) with result kept for the receipt) or add an explicit `outcome` field and have finishCommand mark 'failed' when result.status ∈ {blocked,error,not_placed,busy,proposed}. On iOS decode `result.status`/`result.reasons`/`result.summary` (lossy `[String: AnyCodable]` is enough) and show them in the proposal banner and Activity rows; do the same for run_once summary and order.cancel dustWarning (mobile-api.ts:1004).

#### [api-contract-web-ios:api-03] iOS Scan times out at 25s while the server scan budget is now 35s (#2830 today) — slow scans surface as "Check your connection"
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** iOS <-> GET /api/scan timing contract · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileAPIClient.swift:137`
- **Evidence** /// Interactive market scan — GET `/api/scan`. The server budget is 20s. ... req.timeoutInterval = 25 // vs app/api/scan/route.ts:22 `const INTERACTIVE_SCAN_BUDGET_MS = 35_000;` (was 20_000 until 13b60747a, 2026-08-18)
- **Why it matters** URLRequest.timeoutInterval is an idle timeout and the scan route sends nothing until it finishes, so any scan that legitimately runs 25-35s (Nasdaq 15s timeout + retry + Yahoo whole-set fallback — exactly the path #2830 added) is aborted client-side and shown as a network error ("Check your connection and try again.") while the server keeps working; the next tap coalesces on the single-flight and may time out again. #2830 changed the server without touching the Swift constant or its comment.
- **Recommendation** Raise the iOS scan timeout to server budget + slack (e.g. 45s) and cite the constant; better, have /api/scan return `budgetMs` in the 503/200 body or a response header so the client derives it. Add a vitest that greps MobileAPIClient.swift for the scan timeout and asserts it exceeds INTERACTIVE_SCAN_BUDGET_MS (pattern already exists in test/apns-deep-link-contract.test.ts).

#### [backend-reliability:berel-02] listFillEvents returns the OLDEST 500 fills (ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT 500) so P&L, open lots, tax, model-stats and dashboard silently freeze once an account passes 500 fills
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** performance/fills · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED · **Prior** 2026-07-03 #39
- **Where** `src/lib/db-fills.ts:332`
- **Evidence** .prepare("SELECT * FROM fill_events WHERE account_number = ? AND source = ? AND user_id = ? ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT ?").all(accountNumber, source, userId, limit) // limit = 500
- **Why it matters** Sixteen callers (dashboard.ts:675-676, performance.ts:460-493, tax.ts:337, strategy.ts:801, strategy-tuning.ts:335-418, experience-memory.ts:263, model-stats route:58, robinhood-pnl-crosscheck.ts:60) pass 500. Because the sort is ascending, the newest fills are the ones dropped: after fill #500 the equity/P&L replay, closed-lot scorecards, tax lots and 'prior fills' context the LLM sees stop advancing while the broker keeps trading. Prior review 2026-07-03 #39 called for window disclosure but assumed the window was 'last 500'; the direction makes it 'first 500'. A 15-minute-cadence account reaches 500 fills in weeks.
- **Recommendation** Drop the LIMIT for FIFO replay consumers (fill_events per account is a few thousand rows; the query is indexed by idx_fill_events_account) or select the newest N with a subquery and re-sort ascending, then thread the {windowRowsScanned,totalRowsAvailable} receipt through calculatePnl as #39 asked. Add a regression test that inserts 501 fills and asserts the newest is present.

#### [frontend-performance:dweb-02] getEquityOrders pulls the ENTIRE Alpaca order history (up to 50 pages x 500) on every dashboard snapshot and 20 other call sites, then ships all of it to every client each poll
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** dashboard server cost / broker chain · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/alpaca.ts:491`
- **Evidence** alpaca.ts:491-506 `async getEquityOrders(...) { ... status: "all", limit: 500 ... for (let guard = 0; guard < 50; guard++) { const page = await this.trackHealth(() => this.alpaca.getOrders({ status: "all", limit: PAGE, direction: "desc", ...until }))`
- **Why it matters** computeDashboardSnapshot (dashboard.ts:485-489) calls it inside the portfolio/positions/orders bundle under a 16 s + 8 s retry deadline; the scheduler tick (scheduler.ts:857,884), protective stops, synthetic stops, order-replacement (6 sites) etc. call it too — 20 call sites, none cached. For an account that has been auto-trading for months this is N sequential Alpaca round-trips per snapshot (<=10 s TTL) at the p95 ~3 s/call STATUS.md documents, a likely contributor to the `getPortfolioBundle 8000+7000ms` timeouts that blank portfolio/positions/orders on the dashboard. The full history array (`orders`, dashboard.ts:1106) is then serialized into the snapshot every 15 s and JSON.parsed on th…
- **Recommendation** For the snapshot and scheduler paths fetch `status: "open"` plus `status: "closed", limit: 100` (or `after: <7d>`), one page each, and cache the closed page per account for the snapshot TTL; keep the full paginated walk only behind an explicit `getEquityOrderHistory()` used by a paged `/api/orders?before=` endpoint the Orders page calls on demand. Log page count per call to confirm the effect on the bundle timeouts in prod.

#### [market-data-integrity:mdi-02] Daily-bar cache served as 'fresh' for 3 calendar days: series lags up to two sessions and today's partial bar is persisted as an EOD close
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** price history / benchmark / technicals · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED · **Prior** 2026-08-08 #2557b (symptom gate only)
- **Where** `src/lib/history.ts:246`
- **Evidence** `if (localBars && localBars.length >= 2 && isBarSeriesFresh(localBars, 3, now)) { ... return stampedLocal; }` with isBarSeriesFresh: `ageMs <= maxStalenessDays*24h` on the bar DATE string
- **Why it matters** A Monday 9:35 fetch writes Monday's intraday partial bar into history_cache_eod (line 327 / persistEodBarsToCache) keyed 2026-08-17 (parses to 00:00Z); on Tue and Wed the series is still 'fresh' (<=72h) so no refetch happens until Thursday. Consumers get a stale last 'close' and missing sessions: SPY benchmark (benchmark.ts:345), computed technical signals, /api/history drilldown chart, and Alpaca's after-hours 'last close' quote fallback (alpaca.ts:565-569). The 2026-08-08 #2557b gate only hides >5-day lag.
- **Recommendation** Freshness = last bar date >= previous ET trading day AND (during/after today's session) today's bar refreshed within HISTORY_TTL; never persist the current session's bar as EOD (tag partial and always overwrite it); refetch when local last date < expected last session.

#### [market-data-integrity:mdi-03] Cascade stamps cache-served provider values with a 'now' fetchedAt/asOf; nasdaq-quote price/change/volume are cached 6h and win volume arbitration on fabricated recency
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** enrichment cascade provenance · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/data-providers.ts:1571`
- **Evidence** takeScalar: `fetchedAt: supplied?.fetchedAt ?? cascadeFetchedAt` and asOf falls to `cascadeFetchedAt`; nasdaq-quote: `cache.set(\`nasdaq-quote:${symbol}\`, { expiresAt: now + ttlMs(), data })` (ttlMs = NEWS_CACHE_TTL_MS, 6h)
- **Why it matters** nasdaq-quote's lastSalePrice/percentageChange/volume (parseNasdaqQuoteInfo:2521-2539) come back from a 6-hour cache with no timestamp, and the cascade stamps them as observed 'now'. `volume` arbitration is recency-first (evidence-facts.ts:100-107) so the stale cached volume beats Alpaca's real dailyBar.v with a genuine timestamp; when Alpaca snapshot is absent or times out (today's STATUS: 6s aborts), price/intradayChangePct fall to the hours-old cached value. The fake stamps then flow to symbol_field_latest and the 'Received <time>' tooltips (columns.tsx:22-30).
- **Recommendation** Store fieldObservations (or a writtenAt) in the enrichment cache and reuse them on hits; give price-family fields their own short TTL (like ALPACA_SNAPSHOT 30s) instead of NEWS_CACHE_TTL_MS; make recency compare real provider timestamps only (missing => lowest).

#### [ops-observability-admin:sre-01] No at-a-glance 'is the trader alive' panel on web/admin/iOS; run-state chip is intent, not liveness
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** admin overview / console header / iOS Home · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/admin/page.tsx:99`
- **Evidence** Promise.allSettled([fetch("/api/admin/connections-health"), fetch("/api/admin/llm-usage?sinceDays=30"), fetch("/api/admin/rag-coverage?sinceDays=30"), fetch("/api/admin/server-metrics"), fetch("/api/chat-history?limit=10"), fetch("/api/admin/r2-usage")]);
- **Why it matters** The Operator Overview never reads /api/health or /api/admin/backup-status, so scheduler heartbeat age, trading-liveness (last completed run / consecutive failures), litestream state, release sha and process uptime are absent from the one page meant to be 'server-wide status'. On the console, StateChip/deriveStateInfo (app/console/lib/derive.ts:132-165, chrome.tsx:290) render 'Running · Autopilot' purely from policy.systemState — a stalled tick, a locked run request (today's 2026-08-18 incident) or five failed runs in a row all still read 'Running'. iOS Home shows the same intent-only pill (HomeView.swift:163). The signals exist server-side (getTradingLivenessSummary, scheduler:lastTick) but…
- **Recommendation** Add a first-row 'Engine Health' card to /admin (fetch /api/health public + /api/admin/backup-status): scheduler heartbeat age with warn >5m, per-account last completed run + consecutive failures, litestream state/age, release sha + uptime, last alert delivered. Feed a compact `engineHealth` {schedulerAgeSeconds, tradingLivenessDegraded, lastCompletedRunAt} into DashboardSnapshot/mobile snapshot so StateChip and the iOS pill can downgrade to 'Running · stalled' (warn tone) when heartbeat >5m or liveness degraded. iOS AdminPortalView (WKWebView of /admin) inherits the admin card for free.

#### [performance-accounting:perf-01] Fill ledger keeps the OLDEST 500 fills per source; newest fills silently vanish from every P&L figure
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** performance / fill ledger · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/db-fills.ts:332`
- **Evidence** "SELECT * FROM fill_events WHERE account_number = ? AND source = ? AND user_id = ? ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT ?" (limit 500 everywhere: performance.ts:460-493, dashboard.ts:675, tax.ts:337, outcome-engine.ts:964, strategy.ts:801)
- **Why it matters** listPortfolioSnapshots already had this exact bug fixed ("ASC+LIMIT previously kept the OLDEST slice", db-fills.ts:222) but listFillEvents still returns the first 500 rows chronologically. Once an account passes 500 accounting fills per source (hourly cadence + broker-held stop fills + trims makes this plausible on the paper/Roth books today), every newer exit is dropped: Realized P&L, win rate, avg return, open-lot ledger (Tax), thesis/regime scorecards, decision grading (findRealizedLot), and the strategy's own learning context all freeze at an old state while positions keep closing. Because FIFO needs the full history, a 'newest 500' flip is also wrong.
- **Recommendation** Remove the cap for accounting reads (an account has at most a few thousand rows) or page through all rows; keep a cap only for display lists (performance.fills slice). Add a test that inserts >500 fills and asserts the last sell is realized. Consider a persisted per-symbol lot table so FIFO does not replay history each request.

#### [performance-accounting:perf-03] Short/cover cash sign is inverted in flow inference → phantom deposits/withdrawals on every short trade
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** benchmark / TWR cash-flow inference · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED · **Prior** 2026-08-06 C1 (bounded in PR #2589; root cause still open)
- **Where** `src/lib/cash-flows.ts:164`
- **Evidence** tradeCash += f.side === "sell" || f.side === "cover" ? f.notional : -f.notional; // comment: "buys/shorts reduce cash (negative), sells/covers increase it"
- **Why it matters** At Alpaca (and Tradier) a short sale CREDITS proceeds to cash (equity = cash + long_mv + short_mv, short_mv negative); a cover DEBITS cash. The code treats a short like a buy, so a $3k short yields flow = Δcash − tradeCash = +3k − (−3k) = +$6k 'deposit' and a $3k cover a −$6k 'withdrawal'. On a quiet day the #2557 sanity bound demotes it to 'inferred — unverified' (still shown as a bogus transfer row on Results); on a day the book moved ≥ 2% of the notional it is VERIFIED and TWR is computed against a fake denominator. The owner runs shorts (PG/T on 8/6), so this fires in production. prior_ref 2026-08-06 C1: the phantom-$36.5k mechanism was bounded (#2589) but this sign bug is a live source…
- **Recommendation** tradeCash += (side === 'sell' || side === 'short') ? +notional : −notional (buys and covers consume cash). Add tests: short open / cover with flat equity must infer zero flow.

#### [qa-test-strategy:qa-04] No API-to-iOS snapshot contract test; a server rename silently blanks phone lists
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** Contract tests (mobile snapshot) · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileModels.swift:60`
- **Evidence** pendingProposals = try values.decodeIfPresent([PendingProposal].self, forKey: .pendingProposals) ?? [] ; positions … ?? [] ; iOS test decodes a hand-typed string `private let fullSnapshotJSON` (MobileModelsTests.swift:371); app/api/mobile/snapshot/route.ts GET has zero TS tests
- **Why it matters** The snapshot shape is an inline object literal in the route with no shared type; the only decoder test uses JSON written by hand, so a rename of `pendingProposals`, `recentCommands`, or a nested Proposal field on the server passes every TS and Swift test while the iPhone shows empty proposals/positions (wrong information, not an error).
- **Recommendation** Add test/mobile-snapshot-contract.test.ts that seeds a temp DB + `broker:"test"` account, calls the route GET, asserts the exact top-level key set, and writes/compares a committed fixture test/fixtures/mobile-snapshot.json; add that JSON as a bundle resource of SocraticTradeTests and decode it in MobileModelsTests (replacing the inline string). Reuse the apns-deep-link-contract.test.ts pattern of reading Swift source for CodingKeys.

#### [web-accessibility:a11y-02] Light theme: tone-colored text on soft fills and the Stop/Start button fail WCAG AA (3.0–4.3:1); --con-info fails on every surface
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Cross-surface · **Area** console/tokens · **Effort** M · **Verdict** PARTIAL — Contrast math independently reproduced and confirmed exact: chip-pos #157f4e on soft #e5f1ec = 4.34:1, con-info #0891b2 on white = 3.68:1 (matches expert's numbers precisely via WCAG relative-luminance formula). Real, current-main P1 bug. BUT already fixed on… · **Prior** 2026-08-06 #2561 (1) — still open on main
- **Where** `app/console/console.css:559`
- **Evidence** .con-chip-pos { color: var(--con-pos); background: var(--con-pos-soft); } … .con-chip-info { color: var(--con-info) … } (tokens :40-50: --con-pos #157f4e, --con-neg #d1332e, --con-warn #9a6209, --con-info #0891b2, --con-none #64748b)
- **Why it matters** Computed (WCAG relative luminance, soft = token mixed over #fff): chip pos #157f4e on #e5f1ec = 4.34, neg #d1332e on #faebea = 4.29, warn #9a6209 on #f3ece1 = 4.34, none #64748b on #f0f1f3 = 4.21, info #0891b2 on #e1f2f6 = 3.19 — all at 11px/600 (needs 4.5). Over --con-surface-2 they drop further (3.9–4.0; info 2.97). .con-stop-btn text (neg on neg-soft, :486-490) = 4.29 and .con-start-btn (:494-498) = 4.34 at 12.5px/700 — the money-critical chrome control. --con-info #0891b2 fails even on plain white (3.68) and --con-info-contrast white-on-#0891b2 = 3.68 (:46-47). Reality banner 'NO ACCOUNT' word (--con-none on none-12% wash, :597/:607) = 3.74. Header comment (:19-21) promises AA. Dark pal…
- **Recommendation** Darken the light tone tokens one step and validate against the SOFT fill, e.g. --con-pos #0f6b41, --con-neg #b8281f, --con-warn #7f5008, --con-info #036d8a (also fixes info-contrast), --con-none #56657c; re-run the ratio script against soft@white and soft@surface-2 and against .con-stop-btn/.con-start-btn. Add a unit test that computes these pairs from console.css so regressions fail CI.

#### [desktop-web-ux:dweb-01] Home proposal rows: dead/wrong Approve button, wrong status tones, auto-firing live confirm
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** Home dashboard / proposal lifecycle · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/page.tsx:79`
- **Evidence** decisionFromProposal(`${latest?.runId}-${item.proposal.symbol}-${item.status}`, …) … const isPendingOrFailed = row.status === "pending" || row.status === "failed" || row.status === "blocked";
- **Why it matters** Latest-run rows get a synthetic id, yet the sheet renders a primary 'Approve Proposal' for blocked rows and POSTs /api/proposals/<runId>-<sym>-blocked/approve (404). Real run statuses are proposed/blocked/error (strategy.ts results.push), so chipTone (line 1025) paints 'error' (label 'Failed') and 'proposed' GREEN like filled; the typed live-confirm input auto-submits on onChange match (1080-1084) with no Reject, no LiveTag/BROKER stamp — inconsistent with the Approvals card contract. Also `latest.proposals.slice(0,5).map(...) ?? pending…` (77-80) never falls back when the array is [], so older pending proposals vanish from Home.
- **Recommendation** Make Home rows read-only summaries: drop the Approve path (or route real pending ids to the ApprovalCard/LiveApproveSheet), map tone by isNotPlacedStatus/'error'/'proposed', link to /console/decisions/[id] via row.href, and use `.length ? … : pending` for the fallback.

#### [frontend-performance:dweb-01] /console/connections client bundle ships the server DB layer (SQL schema, migrations, key-store logic): 1.05 MB gz vs ~476 KB for every other console page
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** bundle / server-client boundary · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/settings/brokers.tsx:12`
- **Evidence** brokers.tsx:12 `import { mergeAccountCapabilities } from "@/lib/venue-contract"` -> venue-contract.ts:22 `import { resolveSourceBool } from "./source-settings"` -> source-settings.ts:15-16 `import { LOCAL_USER } from "./db-api-keys"; import { getUserSetting, setUserSetting } from "./db-settings"` (db barrel)
- **Why it matters** Measured on the 2026-08-16 production build in a sibling worktree: `app/console/connections/page-*.js` = 1,063,218 B raw / 285,282 B gz and contains 120 `CREATE TABLE`, 440 `SELECT `, 363 `getDb`, 11 `ENCRYPTION_KEY`, 31 `process.env` references; it also drags in a zod+drizzle+cheerio chunk (729 KB raw / 235 KB gz) and a crypto-browserify polyfill chunk (326 KB raw / 100 KB gz) that no other route loads. Total JS for /console/connections is 1,052,272 B gz vs 475,762 B gz for /console/approvals. The webpack `better-sqlite3: false` alias only stubs the native binding, so all the JS around it (schema, migrations, API-key store, apns) is compiled into the browser bundle: slow page on phone, and…
- **Recommendation** Make `mergeAccountCapabilities`/`knownBrokerLimits` a pure module (move the `publicParked` check in venue-contract.ts:250 behind an injected flag or a separate server-only helper), so brokers.tsx never reaches source-settings/db. Then guard the barrel: add `import "server-only"` to src/lib/db.ts, db-settings.ts, db-api-keys.ts and an eslint `no-restricted-imports` rule for app/console/** -> `@/lib/db*`, `@/lib/source-settings`, `@/lib/server-knobs`. Verify by grepping `.next/static/chunks/app/console/**` for `getDb`/`SELECT ` after `next build`.

#### [ops-observability-admin:sre-03] Backup status can lie: litestream kill-switch markers are invisible to /api/health and Backup Status, and the R2 'writes paused' card ignores the B2 cutover
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** backups / admin overview · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED · **Prior** 2026-08-06 A2
- **Where** `scripts/coolify-prod-start.sh:188`
- **Evidence** LITESTREAM_DISABLE_MARKER="${DATA_DIR}/.litestream-disabled" if [ -f "$LITESTREAM_DISABLE_MARKER" ]; then log "Generic kill-switch marker present ... starting WITHOUT litestream replication"
- **Why it matters** Two markers make the boot script start without litestream (generic .litestream-disabled, owner override 2026-08-13, lines 188-192; R2 .litestream-r2-disabled only when the endpoint is Cloudflare R2, lines 200-212). Neither marker is modeled anywhere in src/ (rg 'litestream-disabled' in src/app returns nothing): /api/health reports litestreamState 'unknown' and pages 'Litestream runtime status is unavailable — replication freshness cannot be confirmed' (route.ts:459-460) every 12h, and /admin/backups shows an unexplained 'unknown' — the true state 'backups deliberately OFF since <date>' is never said. Conversely app/api/admin/r2-usage/route.ts:29 returns replicationDisabled from raw marker e…
- **Recommendation** Add `litestreamPausedBy: 'generic-marker'|'r2-marker'|null` (read the same two paths + endpoint check as the boot script) to runtime-health and surface it in /api/health.checks.storage, /api/admin/backup-status and the Backups page as an explicit 'Replication intentionally off since …' state with its own alert key (daily reminder, not the 'unavailable' text). Make replicationDisabled in r2-usage route = marker && isLitestreamReplicaCloudflareR2(); drop or reword the overview card copy now that the replica is B2.

#### [performance-accounting:perf-02] Home Day P&L is zeroed on ordinary trading days: flow inference runs with NO fills and reads market P&L as a deposit/withdrawal
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** dashboard Day P&L · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/lib/derive.ts:443`
- **Evidence** const flowMap = inferExternalCashFlows([baseline, fakeCurrent], []); ... const pnl = currentEquity - baseline.equity - flow; — cash-flows.ts:204-216: residualIsTransfer = |Δequity| >= max(threshold, 0.25*swapped) → flow = deltaEquity
- **Why it matters** With an empty fill list, tradeCash is 0, so any day with a trade lands in the 'cash and positions moved opposite' branch. Example on a $100k book: buy $2k, market +$1k → Δcash −2k, Δpos +3k, Δequity +1k; swapped=2k, 0.25×2k=500 ≤ 1k → flow=+$1,000 'deposit'; sanity bound (5×|Δequity|=5k) does not catch it → Day P&L renders $0.00 (0.00%) with cashFlowAdjusted=true. Any day where |Δequity| ≥ 0.5% of equity and ≥ 25% of traded notional prints the wrong headline number. The benchmark path passes fills (dashboard.ts:718) so it is mostly immune; deriveDayPnl is not.
- **Recommendation** Pass the snapshot's fills (snapshot.performance.fills — filter to accounting statuses, or extend the summary with the current-day fills) into inferExternalCashFlows in deriveDayPnl; better, use the broker's own previous-close equity (Alpaca account.last_equity) as the Day P&L baseline and drop inference for the day figure. Add a unit test for the buy-$2k/+$1k case.

#### [ux-copy-editor:copy-03] Macro page says below-median buys are "hard-vetoed" in risk-off/crisis regimes; the code is advisory tag-not-drop
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** copy accuracy / Macro · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/macro/indicators.ts:698`
- **Evidence** "VIX above 30 — panic-level volatility. Buy ideas scoring below the scan median are hard-vetoed, ..." (also :706); src/lib/strategy-risk.ts:187-197 tags preVetoReasons and keeps the proposal
- **Why it matters** Wrong information about what the system will do with the owner's money in a crisis regime — the regime rule was converted to an overridable advisory pre-veto (strategy-risk.ts comment: "advisory pre-veto (tag-not-drop). Was a hard `continue` drop"). Also contradicts the owner's "nothing hard but the account boundary" ruling in user-facing copy.
- **Recommendation** Reword both regime hints: "Buy ideas scoring below the scan median are flagged with an advisory veto the agent must override with a thesis (Socratic override mode); the optional crisis exposure cap can shrink every newly opened position." Grep the rest of macro/indicators.ts and settings/help.tsx for 'hard' and align with the advisory reality.

#### [web-accessibility:a11y-01] Home 'Recent decisions' rows are click-only <div>s — details + Approve flow unreachable by keyboard
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** Desktop web · **Area** console/dashboard · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/page.tsx:1029`
- **Evidence** <div className="cursor-pointer hover:bg-… flex items-center justify-between gap-2 …" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}> … View details <ArrowRight size={12} />
- **Why it matters** ProposalRow (page.tsx:968) opens the Proposal Details sheet — which contains the Approve/typed-confirm path (page.tsx:1075+) — from a plain div with no role, tabIndex, or key handler. Keyboard and switch users cannot open it at all; screen readers see static text 'View details'. The row nests a SymbolButton, so the outer element cannot simply become a button.
- **Recommendation** Make 'View details' a real <button type="button"> (or <Btn variant="ghost" size="sm">) inside the row that calls setOpen(true), keep the row-level onClick as a pointer convenience, and give the sheet an aria-labelledby title (already passed).

#### [ios-engineering:ios-01] Sign-out / expiry / account deletion never clears the UserDefaults snapshot cache — next launch boots the previous account's data and mislabels an explicit sign-out as "session expired"
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** iOS · **Area** session/storage · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:149`
- **Evidence** init: `let cached = previewSnapshot ?? Self.loadCachedSnapshot(); snapshot = cached; isAuthenticated = cached != nil` — and clearLocalSession() (628-650) sets snapshot=nil but never `UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: cacheKey)`
- **Why it matters** After `signOut()` (or `confirmAccountDeletion`, or a 401 wipe) the cache row survives, so a cold relaunch derives `isAuthenticated = true` from a stale blob: online, `load()` 401s and shows "Your session expired. Sign in again." to a user who deliberately signed out (shouldReportSessionExpiry is true because isAuthenticated was true); offline, `hasInitialized` flips and MobileControlView renders the previous account's holdings, orders, proposals and email. Same failure after account deletion — the deleted account's portfolio remains on disk.
- **Recommendation** In `clearLocalSession()` (and `signOut`/deletion) `UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: Self.cacheKey)`; do not infer authentication from the cache alone — persist an explicit signed-in flag or check `HTTPCookieStorage.shared` for the session cookie before treating the cache as authenticated; treat a cache-only launch as `isAuthenticated=false` + splash until the first successful load.

#### [ios-hig-ux:ios-01] Guardrail/account edits inside Account & Settings fail silently — store.error is never rendered in the sheet, and the scaffold banner is off-screen or auto-cleared elsewhere
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** iOS · **Area** iOS error surfacing (Settings sheet, Guardrails, Home) · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:766`
- **Evidence** HomeView.swift:766 `Form { identitySection ... GuardrailTighteningSection() ... }` (no store.error view); PolicyTightening.swift:623 `store.error = "Type \(pending.phrase) to confirm."`; MobileStore.swift:292 `error = nil`
- **Why it matters** Every failure from account.activate, policy.patch (cap raise/lower, Ask-First↔Autopilot), the typed-phrase mismatch, and custom-cap validation is written to `store.error`, but AccountSettingsView (a sheet) contains no InlineErrorBanner — the only renderer is SnapshotScaffold (AppComponents.swift:580) which sits at the TOP of the underlying tab's scroll view. On the Guardrails tab the edit card is the LAST card, so the user is scrolled to the bottom when the banner appears at the top. Worse, `load()` sets `error = nil` on every successful refresh and SSE events trigger `scheduleReload()`, so a banner can vanish within seconds. Net effect: the owner taps a money-adjacent change, sees nothing,…
- **Recommendation** Render `store.error` inside AccountSettingsView (a top Section or `.alert(item:)`), and change SnapshotScaffold to surface action errors as an overlay/toast anchored to the safe area (or `.alert`) instead of a card at scroll offset 0. Do not clear an action error in `load()`; clear it only on dismiss/timeout or when the same operation succeeds. Add an XCTest that a failed `policy.patch` leaves a visible error state.

#### [qa-test-strategy:qa-01] iOS unit tests never execute in CI or before TestFlight ship
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** iOS · **Area** CI / iOS test target · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `.github/workflows/ios-build.yml:73`
- **Evidence** xcodebuild build \ -project 'ios/Socratic Trade.xcodeproj' -scheme SocraticTrade -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' — no `xcodebuild test` anywhere in .github/ or scripts/
- **Why it matters** 15 XCTest files (2,416 lines: MobileModels decode, PolicyTightening, OrderCancel, PushNotification, DeepLink) only run when a human remembers; ios-ship.yml archives every 30 min from main without them, so a decode/contract regression ships to the phone.
- **Recommendation** Add a second step to ios-build.yml: `xcodebuild test -project 'ios/Socratic Trade.xcodeproj' -scheme SocraticTrade -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17 Pro' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` (the Mac runner already has simulators), and have scripts/ios-scheduled-ship-gate.sh refuse to archive when the last ios-build test run on that sha is not green.

#### [qa-test-strategy:qa-02] Swift build result is not part of the required merge gate
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** risk · **Surface** iOS · **Area** CI ruleset · **Effort** M · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `.github/workflows/ci.yml:305`
- **Evidence** ruleset main-protection: required_status_checks:[{context:"verify"}]; verify job needs:[classify, verify-hosted] only — ios-build.yml runs separately and is advisory
- **Why it matters** AGENTS.md documents a merge that produced a duplicate `@Binding pendingDeepLink` init and did not compile while the full green gate passed (2026-08-13). Today the same class merges again because a red `xcodebuild (unsigned)` cannot block.
- **Recommendation** Add `ios-changed` to the classify job outputs (git diff --name-only ... | grep '^ios/'), move the xcodebuild job into ci.yml gated on it, and make the `verify` gate step read its result: skipped-because-unchanged = pass, failure = fail. Keeps non-iOS PRs off the Mac runner while making iOS breakage un-mergeable.

#### [trading-money-path:tsx-03] iOS shows 'Approved — waiting for desk refresh' (green check) for approvals that returned busy/blocked/rejected_by_broker/not_placed/error
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** iOS · **Area** approval flow feedback · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `src/lib/mobile-api.ts:966`
- **Evidence** case "proposal.approve": return executeProposal(String(payload.proposalId), command.userId, {...}); … return toPublicMobileCommand(finishCommand(command, "succeeded", result)); (:1080)
- **Why it matters** executeProposal RETURNS (does not throw) objects like {status:'busy'} (strategy lock held by a running cycle), {status:'blocked'}, {status:'error'} (broker declined), {status:'not_placed'}. runCommand passes them straight to finishCommand('succeeded'), so ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:214 maps the command to .succeeded and ProposalsView.swift:481 renders 'Approved — waiting for desk refresh.' The phone user believes a live order went in when nothing was placed; the only truth is buried in Activity. Console handles the same statuses correctly (approval-card.tsx:347-366).
- **Recommendation** In runCommand('proposal.approve'), inspect result.status: 'placed'/'filled'/'paper'/'proposed' → succeeded; 'busy'/'blocked'/'error'/'not_placed'/'rejected_by_broker' → finishCommand('failed', result, reasons.join(' ')). Mirror the console's finish() wording in the failed message. Add a mobile-api test for the busy case.

#### [ux-copy-editor:copy-04] iOS tells the user to edit the universe "on the Strategy page" — the app has no Strategy screen and on the web the universe editor lives under Guardrails
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** bug · **Surface** iOS · **Area** copy accuracy / first-run setup · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:94`
- **Evidence** ? "Include an index or symbols on the Strategy page." (also HomeView.swift:109, GuardrailsView.swift:86 "Universe edits stay on the Strategy page."); web editor: app/console/guardrails/page.tsx:425 <AdvancedGroup title="Universe">, derive.ts:707 ctaLabel "Open Guardrails · Universe"
- **Why it matters** The iOS setup checklist and Guardrails screen misdirect a first-run user to a screen that does not exist in the app and to the wrong page on the website (Strategy has models/prompts/presets; the universe is on Guardrails → Advanced rulebook → Universe). Wayfinding is broken exactly at the step that blocks the first run.
- **Recommendation** Change all three strings to name the real location and surface: "Include an index or symbols on the website under Guardrails → Universe (socratictrade.com/console/guardrails)." Consider a deep link/button that opens that URL in Safari or the in-app web view.

#### [mobile-web-ux:mweb-01] Phone chrome bar over-budget since run-state label became "Stop Agent"/"Resume Agent" — account scope squeezed to ~0-15px at 360-390px
- **Severity** P1 · **Kind** defect · **Surface** Mobile web · **Area** console chrome bar (shell.tsx ChromeBar / chrome.tsx ScopeSelector, StateChip, RunStateButton) · **Effort** S · **Verdict** CONFIRMED
- **Where** `app/console/components/chrome.tsx:326`
- **Evidence** const label = state === "halted" ? "Start Agent" : state === "close_only" ? "Resume Agent" : "Stop Agent"; // chrome.tsx:203 still says "at 360px the leftover is ~73px"
- **Why it matters** The phone bar was width-tuned when the run-state control was a short STOP/Start (#1708 comment at chrome.tsx:199-205: scope is min-w-0 flex-1 and takes whatever is left). #2757 (2026-08-16) lengthened the label to "Stop Agent"/"Resume Agent" (~125-140px incl. icon+padding, flex-shrink:0 in .con-stop-btn), while the coarse-pointer rule gives Run-once 44x44 and StateChip stacks "Paused"/"market closed" (~90px), plus 32px cmdK + 32px avatar + gaps/padding (~54px). Fixed widths sum to ~375px, so at 360px the bar overflows and at 390px (iPhone 14/15) the account scope — the ONE control that says which brokerage account is loaded — gets ~13px and truncates to nothing. For a multi-account trader t…
- **Recommendation** On phones drop the noun: render `Stop`/`Start`/`Resume` below sm (`<span className="hidden sm:inline"> Agent</span>`) or icon-only with aria-label, and give ScopeSelector a small min-w floor (e.g. 84px) so the account label always shows at least a broker name + last-4. Add a 360px/390px width check to the phone-chrome test.

- **[backend-reliability:berel-08] Scheduler tick has no re-entrancy guard: setInterval fires every 60 s while a tick can await serial per-account broker health calls and then all strategy runs, so ticks overlap and unguarded per-tick lanes stack under exactly the slow-broker conditions of 08-18** — `src/lib/scheduler.ts:411` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED

- **[ops-observability-admin:sre-08] All app + litestream stdout is appended forever to a log file on the persistent data volume with no rotation** — `scripts/coolify-prod-start.sh:223` · risk · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED

- **[performance-accounting:perf-08] Drawdown breaker HWM is not flow-adjusted and its 'day' is UTC — withdrawals/paper resets read as drawdown, daily-loss window resets at 7pm CT** — `src/lib/risk-breaker.ts:86` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: A $20k withdrawal (or Alpaca paper reset) drops equity below the persisted HWM forever, so the advisory/kill-switch reports 'Trailing drawdown 20%' on no market loss and, with drawdownBreakerAction set, flips to close_only. Start-of-day equity keyed on the UTC date makes 'today's loss' span 7pm CT→7pm CT, unlike every other daily boundary in the app (Central).

- **[performance-accounting:perf-09] Decision outcome grading uses only the FIRST partial exit of a lot (won/lost/pnlUsd wrong for scaled-out positions)** — `src/lib/outcome-engine.ts:966` · bug · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: calculatePnl emits one ClosedLot per (lot, closing fill) pair; a take-profit trim at +2% followed by the remainder stopped out at −8% grades the decision 'won' with the trim's P&L, and terminates the case early while most of the position is still open. Feeds lessons and Decisions pages with wrong verdicts.
  - Fix: Aggregate all closed lots with the same entryAt (sum pnl, capital-weighted returnPct) and only mark terminal when the entry lot is fully closed (compare closed qty to entry fill qty).

- **[performance-accounting:perf-10] Synthetic 20 bps 'paper' execution-cost haircut is applied to REAL Alpaca-paper fill prices, but only when the price is known at booking — same account mixes shaded and raw fills** — `src/lib/performance.ts:244` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED

Fills that return an averagePrice immediately are booked net of a modeled cost; fills reconciled later are overwritten with the raw broker truth. Realized P&L on the paper book therefore neither equals the broker's nor is uniformly net-of-model, and Result…
  - Fix: Book price = broker price always; keep the modeled cost in a separate FillEvent field (modeledCostBps) used only by the learning loop's net-of-cost stats, or disable PAPER_EXECUTION_COST_MODEL by default now that paper = broker.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-04] Any HTTP 4xx from the broker — including 429 rate-limit and 408 — is filed as a definitive terminal rejected_by_broker without a refId check** — `src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:1279` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: formatAlpacaOrderError prefixes `HTTP ${status}`; Alpaca returns 429 when the 200 req/min budget is exhausted (see tsx-05 for why that is realistic) and the SDK does not retry. The proposal is retired as 'Rejected by broker' — wrong information to the trader, and unlike not_placed it never says 'safe to retry'. Same regex at strategy.ts:3979.
  - Fix: Narrow the terminal branch to 400/403/422 (and 404 on cancel), route 408/425/429/409 through reconcilePlacementError (or straight to not_placed with the retry hint), and put the status code in the audit payload.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-05] Alpaca getEquityOrders walks the ENTIRE order history (up to 50×500 pages) on every call, and 3+ scheduler lanes call it every 60s per account** — `src/lib/alpaca.ts:500` · risk · Backend · effort M · CONFIRMED
