Daily protective-stop replacement grows history without bound, so cost is O(history) round-trips per call — 6+ full walks/minute across Roth+Paper. This is consistent with the STATUS.md 6-8s getAccount…
  - Fix: Add getOpenEquityOrders() (status open, one page) for coverage/stale checks, and bound the 'all' walk with `after` = oldest pending fill/placing intent created_at minus a day; cache the list per tick across lanes (the tick already holds a per-account lease). Keep the full walk only for explicit forensics.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-06] Alpaca getEquityTradability is a stub (tradable:true, fractional:true for every symbol) — shortable/fractionable/easy_to_borrow are never checked pre-flight** — `src/lib/alpaca.ts:582` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: executeProposal (:483) and the 'not tradable' hard gate rely on this. On Alpaca a short on a non-shortable/HTB name, a dollar order on a non-fractionable asset, or a halted/inactive asset only fails at the broker (403/422 → rejected_by_broker) after the owner already typed the live confirmation, and the LLM keeps proposing the same names.
  - Fix: Call GET /v2/assets/{symbol} (cache 1h): map tradable, fractionable, shortable, easy_to_borrow, status==='active'; return fractional=false so the notional→qty path floors correctly, and reason strings for the block. Feed shortable into the strategy prompt's short candidates.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-09] Approval-time ratio re-anchoring moves EXIT limits with the market — a held take-profit sell at $110 (ref $100) is placed at $99 if the quote fell to $90; entries chase upward inside the drift cap** — `src/lib/approval-reprice.ts:100` · risk · Backend · effort S · PARTIAL — Behaviour confirmed: approval-reprice.ts:99-103 ratio-re-anchors any-side limits including sell/cover, and strategy-execution.ts:434-449 only routes drift back to the human for live+typed material drift or opening-side entry-drift-cap breach — so on paper (or…
  - Why: A profit-taking or manual sell limit is a price LEVEL, not an aggressiveness ratio. On broker/paper, or live with requireTypedConfirmation=false, drift is not routed back to the human (strategy-execution.ts:434-472 gates only live+typed and opening-side drift), so the order placed is materially different from the one reviewed and the sell can execute below the intended target.…
  - Fix: Exempt sell/cover limits from ratio re-anchoring unless the proposal is a protective exit (already owned by protective-exit-routing) or the owner opts in; for opening limits keep it but treat 'material' drift as re-approval on every mode when the card is human-approved (owner is clicking anyway).

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-11] Hard-coded 5% opening-order headroom gate shrinks the owner's own per-order cap with no policy toggle — a human-approved $490 order against a $500 maxOrderNotional is blocked at approval** — `src/lib/policy.ts:567` · defect · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling: guardrails are adjustable preferences with an easy off-switch. This one is a compile-time constant that turns the configured cap into 95% of itself; the LLM is told about it (strategy-prompts.ts:178) but chat/from-draft/manual proposals and broker-minimum bumps hit it as a surprise block, and the only 'override' is to raise the cap the owner deliberately set.
  - Fix: Make it policy.openingOrderHeadroomPct (default 5, 0 = off) exposed on Guardrails, and treat it as advisory (escalatable) rather than a block on the human-approval path.

- **[api-contract-web-ios:api-04] strategy.run_once stores the full StrategyResult (marketScan.quotesBySymbol, topCandidates, llmSteps) in mobile_commands.result and ships it in every /api/mobile/snapshot recentCommands[]** — `src/lib/mobile-api.ts:955` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The strategy_run audit deliberately omits the full MarketScan (scan/route.ts comment) because it is large, yet the mobile command row persists it whole and the phone downloads up to 30 such blobs on every SSE-triggered reload, then writes the whole snapshot into UserDefaults (MobileStore.swift:143 saveCachedSnapshot). iOS never decodes `result`. performance.fills also carries…
  - Fix: Persist a compact result for run_once (runId, status, summary, proposal count/ids) — reuse summarizeResult-style shaping before finishCommand — or strip marketScan/llmSteps in toPublicMobileCommand; omit `result` from snapshot recentCommands (keep it on GET /api/mobile/commands/:id); drop `raw` from fills in the mobile projection.

- **[api-contract-web-ios:api-06] No generated contract fixtures between server routes and Swift decoders — hand-mirrored enums/constants/JSON drift silently** — `ios/SocraticTradeTests/MobileModelsTests.swift:6` · risk · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: api-01 and api-03 are both drift the compiler cannot see. Other hand mirrors: OrderCancel.swift:29-49 vs broker-held-orders.ts:9-25,54 (order states); PolicyTightening.swift:24 `maxOrderNotionalCeiling = 100_000` vs mobile-api.ts:335; DeskModels.swift:176-184 coach model ids vs app/ui/llm-model-catalog.ts; DeskModels.swift:482 group keys vs source-settings-catalog.ts. The repo…
  - Fix: Add a vitest that boots the routes against a seeded temp DB and writes golden JSON (snapshot, policy, scan, quote, symbol-desk, chat-history, llm-budget, source-features) into ios/SocraticTradeTests/Fixtures/, and Swift tests that decode those files; extend apns-deep-link-contract.test.ts to assert the mirrored sets/constants match their TS sources.

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-05] After acknowledge/cancel/run-once, `await refresh()` is served from the 10 s snapshot cache, so the acked alert / cancelled order stays on screen** — `app/console/components/alert-center.tsx:298` · bug · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior docs/rollouts/2026-08-04-ux-wave-c-speed.md C1
  - Why: getDashboardSnapshot (dashboard.ts:340) returns the cached entry for `DASHBOARD_SNAPSHOT_TTL_MS = 10_000` (dashboard-snapshot-cache.ts:13). Foreground `refresh()` (useConsoleData.tsx:203-207) calls the same GET with no bypass, so a user-initiated mutation whose route does not invalidate (notifications ack, order cancel/replace-market, strategy run request, account activate if…
  - Fix: Either (a) call `invalidateDashboardSnapshotCache(userId)` in every mutating route under app/api that touches snapshot material (notifications/ack, orders/cancel, orders/replace-market, strategy/run|pause|enable, connected-accounts/*/activate), or (b) make foreground `refresh()` send `?fresh=1` and have /api/dashboard invalidate the key before computing. Add a test asserting t…

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-07] TickerLogo starts as theme=dark and flips to light after mount, so every logo in the light-default console requests the dark variant first (double fetch + wrong-variant flash), plus a MutationObserver/matchMedia listener per instance** — `app/console/ui/ticker-logo.tsx:21` · bug · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling: default theme is light. Every `<TickerLogo>` (used by SymbolButton in positions/orders/activity/scan rows and drawers) commits an `<img>` with `theme=dark`, then the effect reads `.console-root[data-theme="light"]` and re-commits `theme=light`: two image URLs, two server hits, two logo.dev fetches on a cold disk cache, and a dark tile that flashes to light — repe…
  - Fix: Initialise theme from the nearest `.console-root[data-theme]` synchronously (`useSyncExternalStore` or a tiny ThemeContext provided by ShellFrame that already knows `dataTheme`), default `"light"` when unknown, and hoist the observer into that single provider. Also add `sizes`/explicit width+height so lazy logos do not shift rows.

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-08] /console/watchlist and /console/lessons fetch their own data but still wait behind the full-screen snapshot loader (up to ~24 s worst case)** — `app/console/components/shell.tsx:67` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The shell's own comment says routes that consume zero snapshot fields should be allow-listed so they paint immediately, but only /console/usage is. Watchlist (own /api/watchlist + /api/alerts poll, 30 s) and Lessons (own /api/learned-context poll, 60 s) render nothing until /api/dashboard returns — on a cold phone load that is the whole broker chain (16 s + 8 s retry bundle pe…
  - Fix: Add "/console/watchlist" and "/console/lessons" to SNAPSHOT_INDEPENDENT_ROUTES (learned-context's `refresh` is safe pre-snapshot); consider SELF_SKELETON for /console/scan (own live scan) and /console/assistant, and prefer a route-level `snapshotRequired` export over a hard-coded Set so new pages opt in explicitly.

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-06] 'Retry Red Team' stamps approve-at-half / reject verdicts without applying the haircut or hold — approving the card places FULL size** — `src/lib/retry-red-team.ts:47` · bug · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The run-loop path (strategy.ts:2659-2697) applies applyRedTeamHalfSize or holds for human when half is unplaceable, and rejects drop the proposal; the retry path (console card + iOS via mobile-api.ts:39) only stamps the verdict. The card then shows 'AI critic: half size' (approval-card.tsx:201-206) beside the unchanged full notional, and executeProposal only re-reviews/haircut…
  - Fix: On retry: pass {context, sizing} like the run loop, apply applyRedTeamHalfSize on approve-at-half (or requireHumanReview with the half-unplaceable note), and on reject set the same rejected reasons/decision the loop uses; surface the size delta on the card.

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-11] Under rotation the approval card asserts 'this run's rotation pick, not a failover' even when an implicit fallback (the cheapest seats) actually served the proposal** — `app/console/components/approval-card.tsx:127` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Issue #2577 added implicit failovers for rotating Green seats; implicitGreenRotationFallbacks prefers gemini-flash/mistral-medium/flash-lite/mistral-small (model-rotation.ts:90-95, 184-197). When the pick 400s/times out the run is served by one of those, but only bullFailoverNote/strategy_llm_failover (run record + audit) know it — the proposal carries just proposedByModel, so…
  - Fix: Persist greenServedByFallback {fromModel, reason} on the proposal at strategy.ts:6212-6230 and render 'rotation pick X failed (HTTP 400); served by fallback Y' on the card + iOS ProposalsView.

- **[market-data-integrity:mdi-05] Alpaca broker quote 'price' is the ASK; it becomes the displayed Price, watchlist price, alert trigger and stop/take-profit mark, while Chg% stays the screener's** — `src/lib/alpaca.ts:551` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: mergeQuoteData (app/api/scan/route.ts:102, strategy.ts:771) replaces `price` with the ask but keeps intradayChangePct (market.ts:1129-1141), so a row shows an ask-priced 'Price' next to a change computed from a different source/time; the watchlist (api/watchlist/route.ts:20 -> page.tsx:253) and price alerts show/evaluate the ask; currentPricesFromScan (strategy.ts:782 -> gener…
  - Fix: Use mid (or latest trade from the snapshot endpoint) as `price`, keep bid/ask separately, recompute intradayChangePct from prevClose when price is replaced (or clear/relabel it), and show 'ask'/'mid' in the tooltip.

- **[market-data-integrity:mdi-09] 'volume' mixes session, 10-day average and IEX-only volume across providers, feeding liquidity score, dollar-volume floor and outlier detection** — `src/lib/data-providers.ts:2143` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-07-20 #48
  - Why: Robinhood prefers AVERAGE volume even during the session; Alpaca's default IEX feed reports only IEX-printed volume (a small fraction of consolidated); all land in MarketQuote.volume used by liquidityScore (market.ts:1701-1702), passesUniverseFloor dollar volume (145-147), the mean+2sigma volCut (435-441) and ADV caps — apples vs oranges across symbols in one ranking. Prior 20…
  - Fix: Add `averageVolume` as its own field; only session volume may populate `volume`; label IEX-only volume in provenance and prefer consolidated (Nasdaq quote / Yahoo) for floor and ADV.

- **[market-data-integrity:mdi-11] History cascade merges split-only, split+dividend and IEX-only bars into one series/cache without tagging the adjustment basis** — `src/lib/history.ts:611` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Whichever tier answers determines whether closes are total-return or price-return; mergeOHLCBars (131-160) and upsertHistoryCacheEod then splice bars of different bases by date, creating discontinuities at ex-dividend dates. The SPY benchmark (benchmark.ts) therefore compares account equity to SPY total return on some days and price return on others, and indicators computed ov…
  - Fix: Tag OHLCBar.adjustment ('split'|'total'), choose one basis per consumer (split-only for charts/indicators; total-return for benchmark), request it explicitly from each provider, and never merge/persist bars of a different basis into an existing series.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-03] Choosing "System" theme never survives a reload — it is stored by deleting the key, and a missing key reads back as Light** — `app/console/lib/useConsoleTheme.ts:37` · bug · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The default-light change (#2628) made "missing" mean Light, but `set("system")` still encodes System as "missing". So the System option in the phone profile menu (chrome.tsx:928-949) appears to work until the next page load, when it silently reverts to Light — a control that lies.
  - Fix: Persist the literal string "system" (`localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next)` for all three values) and treat only a truly absent key as Light. Add a unit test for the round trip.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-04] ⌘K "Toggle theme" writes localStorage but does not change the visible theme (separate hook instance from the shell)** — `app/console/components/command-palette.tsx:68` · bug · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: useConsoleTheme keeps state in a per-instance useState; the shell's data-theme (shell.tsx:84) is a different instance, so cycling from the palette only mutates the palette's private state + storage. Nothing visible happens until reload — and because of mweb-03 the "system" step then reads back as light. The palette trigger is deliberately always visible on phones (shell.tsx:40…
  - Fix: Lift theme state into a context provided by ShellFrame (or broadcast a `console:theme` event / `storage`-style subscription) so all consumers share one source; have the palette call the shell's `set`.

- **[ops-observability-admin:sre-11] An overdue next run is never flagged: web says 'Next run now' forever, iOS says 'Next Run 12 minutes ago'** — `app/console/lib/format.ts:123` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: scheduler.nextRunAt is an in-memory schedule (scheduler.ts:378-386); when the tick stalls or a run request is locked (2026-08-18 STATUS: request stuck 'running', no strategy_run created for 17m+), the timestamp passes and the console footer (chrome.tsx:1099) and dashboard card (page.tsx:439) keep printing 'Next run now', while iOS HomeView.swift:666-667 prints a relative past…
  - Fix: In timeUntil/next-run rendering, when now − nextRunAt > max(2 min, cadence/2) render 'overdue by 12m' in warn tone with a tooltip pointing to Activity; on iOS compute the same and swap the LabeledContent value color; both should link to the run-lock/heartbeat detail once sre-01 lands.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-04] 'Versus the market' TWR silently covers only the last ~100 snapshots (≈7 trading days) but is framed as account performance** — `src/lib/performance.ts:497` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · PARTIAL — True mechanism: performance.ts:497-498 `listPortfolioSnapshots(..., 100)`, two snapshots per run (strategy.ts:814 pre-run, 4334 post-run) at 60-min default cadence (defaults.ts:164) ⇒ ~7 sessions after per-date collapse. Overstated on labeling: results/page.t…
  - Why: The equity curve behind Results 'Your account / SPY / vs SPY' and iOS 'vs SPY' is a rolling ~1–2 week window whose start date drifts daily (the 8/6 review saw 7/29→8/6). Nothing on the page says 'trailing 7 days'; the tooltip describes a since-inception TWR. A GIPS-minded reader would call this an unlabeled trailing window with a moving start. Also affects the Results BucketCa…
  - Fix: Store/collapse one EOD snapshot per date (SQL: latest row per date) with a large limit so the curve is since-inception, and offer explicit windows (1W / 1M / 3M / YTD / All) with the window named in the label. Until then, put 'trailing N sessions (since <date>)' in the Stat sub-label instead of only the raw dates.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-05] 'Unrealized P&L' / 'Realized P&L' mean different things on Results (lot ledger), Home (broker cost) and iOS (broker positions) — none labels its basis** — `app/console/results/page.tsx:962` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 C3 (ledger mismatch now flagged for Tax only, PR #2589)
  - Why: For an account with pre-app holdings or a drifted ledger (2026-08-06 C3), the three surfaces show three different 'Unrealized P&L' numbers for the same account and none says 'app-booked lots only' vs 'broker cost basis'. Realized P&L on Results is all-time and app-booked (excludes exits of positions the app never opened), while the Tax card is tax-year and the SPY card is ~7 d…
  - Fix: Pick one definition per surface and label it: 'Unrealized (broker cost basis)' everywhere for the account figure; keep the lot-ledger figure only in the tax/lots section labeled 'app-booked lots'. Add 'all-time, app-booked trades' under Realized P&L and the window under each card title.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-06] Win rate '0%' and Avg return '+0.00%' render for accounts with zero closed lots (web + iOS)** — `app/console/results/page.tsx:957` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Any active account has snapshots, so hasAny is effectively always true; winRate() returns 0 for an empty lot list (performance.ts:1708) and iOS has no gate at all, so a fresh or all-open book shows a fabricated 0% win rate next to '—' policy elsewhere ('No closed trades to score yet').
  - Fix: Carry closedLotCount in PerformanceSummary and render '—' (web) / '—' (iOS) until n ≥ 1; show 'n=…' beside win rate.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-11] Win rate and 'n' count partial-close lots, not round trips — trims systematically inflate wins and sample counts** — `src/lib/performance.ts:1707` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: One position trimmed twice at a profit then stopped out counts as 2 wins / 1 loss (67%) and n=3 on Results 'By thesis' and Home 'Closed thesis samples', while the round trip lost money. Shrinkage priors do not fix a biased unit of account.
  - Fix: Report per round-trip (group ClosedLots by entryRunId+symbol or by position episode) for win rate/n, and label the tile 'closed lots' if lots are kept.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-12] Closing fills with no matching app lot are silently dropped — exits of pre-app/manual positions never realize P&L, and nothing surfaces the count** — `src/lib/performance.ts:599` · risk · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 C3 (flagging only, PR #2589)
  - Why: The Roth account holds positions the app did not open; when the strategy sells them the sell is booked but produces $0 realized and disappears from win rate/avg return, and the lot ledger drifts (C3 symptoms: AXP lot with no position). Tax now flags mismatched symbols but Realized P&L / win rate carry no indicator.
  - Fix: Seed opening-balance lots from broker positions (avg_entry_price, qty) at connect time, labeled 'broker basis'; count and display 'unmatched closing fills: N' on Results when any sell/cover found no lot.

- **[public-site-growth:dpub-01] Session-expired redirect drops the destination: deep links (push/email → /console/approvals?proposal=…) land on the dashboard after sign-in** — `middleware.ts:417` · bug · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Every notification deep link (approvals, fills, price alerts, run_failed) points at a /console path. When the 30-day JWT has expired on the phone browser or desktop, the owner is bounced to /login and, after OAuth, to `/` → /console — the proposal/order they tapped is gone and they must re-find it. Neither the middleware nor the login server actions carry the original path.
  - Fix: In middleware append `?callbackUrl=<pathname+search>` (same-origin relative only) on the /login redirect; in app/login/page.tsx read `searchParams.callbackUrl`, validate it starts with `/` and not `//`, and pass it as `redirectTo` to each `signIn(...)` call (default `/console`). Add a middleware-auth test asserting the redirect preserves `/console/approvals?proposal=x`.

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-06] Browser coverage is one 18-line desktop smoke on `/`, not run on PRs, no phone width** — `test/e2e/dashboard-smoke.spec.ts:3` · defect · Cross-surface · effort L · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The owner uses the console daily at desktop AND phone widths, yet no automated run ever loads /console/approvals, /console/orders, /console/strategy (1,522 lines), /console/results or any /admin page in a browser, and none at 390px. A client-side crash on a subroute reaches production before anyone sees it; nightly smoke only says the home shell painted.
  - Fix: Add a `phone` Playwright project (devices["iPhone 15"]), a route-crawl spec that visits every app/console/**/page.tsx and app/admin/** and fails on `Application error`/pageerror/console.error, and one interaction spec (seed a `broker:"test"` account through the API, open Approvals, approve, assert the placed state). Run e2e on PRs whose diff touches app/console/** or app/admin…

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-11] land.sh skips lint and never compiles Swift for ios/** diffs** — `scripts/land.sh:156` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: A lint error (the only rule class CI fails on) is discovered only after push, costing a full ~20-minute CI cycle; an ios/** change is first compiled in CI (AGENTS.md caution) even though the landing machine is the Mac with Xcode.
  - Fix: Add `npm run lint` as step 0 in land.sh, and when `git diff --name-only origin/main...HEAD | grep -q '^ios/'` run the AGENTS.md xcodebuild build (and `xcodebuild test`) before pushing.

- **[security-auth:sec-04] Sessions cannot be revoked: 30-day stateless JWT, sign-out is cookie-delete only, no 'sign out everywhere', no absolute lifetime** — `src/lib/auth/session-token.ts:6` · risk · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: A stolen/leaked cookie (lost phone, laptop left unlocked, WKWebView copy, log capture) stays valid for up to 30 days and can approve trades, change guardrails, and read broker positions; the only remediation is rotating AUTH_SECRET, which logs out every device. Auth.js rolling refresh means an active session never actually expires. The plumbing for a fix already exists: middle…
  - Fix: Store a per-user `sessions_valid_after` timestamp (settings row); in resolveRequestUser reject sessions whose loginAt (or iat for legacy cookies) predates it. Expose 'Sign out of all devices' on /console/settings and in the iOS Settings sheet (also called by account deletion). Add an absolute cap (e.g. force re-login after 30 days from loginAt regardless of refresh) — a prefer…

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-08] Manually cancelling an app-managed protective stop from the console/iOS is silently undone next tick — the reconciler deletes the row and places a fresh stop; the cancel sheet promises 'No new order is placed'** — `src/lib/order-cancel.ts:198` · defect · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-14] Stale-exit remediation on live spams 'stale_exit_auto_remediation_deferred' every 60s per resting sell limit and pushes a misleading 'limit order still working — cancel/reprice before replacing with market' alert for bracket take-profit legs** — `src/lib/order-replacement.ts:763` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Same root as tsx-02: activated bracket TP legs and GTC sells are 'stale' forever, so on the Roth every such order writes ~1,440 audit rows/day (prior review C7/C8 already flagged Activity flooding) and stale-limit-orders.ts:74-78 sends one push per order telling the owner to cancel a take-profit that is resting by design.

- **[ux-copy-editor:copy-02] Double ASCII spaces typed into JSX text collapse to one space at render — looks compliant in source, isn't on screen** — `app/console/components/consent-gate.tsx:107` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED

- **[ux-copy-editor:copy-05] "Approvals" vs "Proposals": nav/H1/iOS tab say Proposals, but a dozen copy strings send users to "Approvals"** — `app/console/assistant/draft-card.tsx:343` · defect · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-09] Late (post-soft-timeout) LLM responses are audited but never metered; Red Team late responses are not captured at all — the Usage page and budget ledger miss the most expensive calls** — `src/lib/strategy.ts:6594` · defect · Desktop web · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-07-20 grok #19
  - Why: llmFetchCapturing lets the request run to hardCap (up to 300s, llm-request.ts:458) after the soft timeout; the provider bills the full reasoning completion, then the fallback is metered on top. Only the fallback's spend reaches llm_usage/the daily budget, so timeouts on frontier reasoning seats (exactly the costly case) are invisible in /console/usage and to checkLlmDailyBudge…
  - Fix: In recordLlmOutcome's late branch call recordLlmUsage(context 'strategy-late') with the extracted usage; give the Red Team fetch an onOutcome that does the same. Show 'late/abandoned' rows on the Usage page.

  - Fix: Route Coach through resolveLlmEndpoint-style precedence (OpenRouter key first, native second) and report 'openrouter' in /api/chat/providers; add llama→meta to chatProviderForModel or reuse llmModelFamily.

  - Fix: Rewrite: 'Reviews every risk-adding opening (buy/short) after sizing; exits are never reviewed. Blank = fail closed → human review.' Add to the fallback hint: 'When the Green seat rotates and this is blank, the run tries up to 2 other eligible pool models.'

- **[performance-accounting:perf-07] Tax 'Short-term realized' silently excludes every covered short (short-side lots skipped)** — `src/lib/tax.ts:352` · bug · Desktop web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: A closed short is a realized (always short-term) gain/loss; the Tax card's ST realized, estimated liability, and wash-sale figures omit them entirely, so a shorting account under-reports taxable results. Estimates-only label does not excuse a systematic omission.
  - Fix: Include side==='short' lots as short-term realized (holding period does not matter for shorts); apply the same loss/wash logic to short covers at a loss.

- **[api-contract-web-ios:api-07] Snapshot decode is all-or-nothing: one malformed element in any list blanks the whole iOS app** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileModels.swift:58` · risk · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: decodeIfPresent throws (not nil) on a wrong-shaped element, so a single NaN→null number or missing timestamp anywhere in positions/orders/fills/alerts/recentCommands turns into MobileAPIError.decoding → "Couldn't load your workspace" with no way to see the desk. The comment on `catalog` shows the team already hit this class once.
  - Fix: Decode list sections with a lossy array wrapper (skip and log bad elements) and wrap `performance`/`benchmark` in try?; keep `readiness`/`policy` strict. Pair with a server-side schema assertion (api-06).

- **[ios-engineering:ios-03] 3-minute staleness gate disables Approve/Run/Watchlist/Policy after any quiet 3 minutes because there is no polling fallback and SSE only fires on demand-driven events** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:223` · defect · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: `lastUpdatedAt` only advances on `load()`, which runs on scene-active, after a command, or on an SSE payload event; the events endpoint emits nothing on an idle desk (`: ping` heartbeats only — app/api/mobile/events/route.ts:41). Reading rationales for 3 minutes on a quiet evening then tapping Approve or Add to Watchlist is refused and the pill turns "Stale". Owner ruling: gua…
  - Fix: Refresh-then-act: when `canSubmit` fails only on staleness, `await load()` and re-check before submitting instead of refusing; add a light foreground poll (every 60–120 s while `.active` and no SSE payload in the last N s) so `lastUpdatedAt` stays honest; keep the pill.

- **[ios-engineering:ios-05] Cold-start push tap can be dropped: `route` is nil until ContentView's `.task` runs `configure(route:)`, and `didReceive` calls `self.route?(destination)` with no buffering** — `ios/SocraticTrade/PushNotifications.swift:441` · risk · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: For a launch-from-notification tap, UNUserNotificationCenter delivers `didReceive` right after `didFinishLaunching`; the SwiftUI `.task` that installs the router runs after first render. Both land on the main actor with no ordering guarantee, so the destination can be silently discarded and the user lands on Home instead of the proposal — the exact scenario the PushAppDelegate…
  - Fix: Store a `pendingDestination` in the coordinator when `route == nil` and flush it inside `configure(route:)`; add a unit test that calls `didReceive` before `configure` and asserts the destination is delivered.

- **[ios-engineering:ios-09] No CI job runs the Swift test suite — ios-build.yml compiles only, the ship script archives only; 15 XCTest files (~2.4k lines) never execute anywhere but a developer's Mac** — `.github/workflows/ios-build.yml:72` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: DeepLinkTests, PushNotificationTests (the deep-link contract the server test pins against), OrderCancelTests, PolicyTighteningTests and MobileModelsTests guard money-adjacent behavior, yet the required `verify` gate is TS-only and the iOS gate is compile-only; a regression in the Swift routing/decoding tables merges green. ios/CLAUDE.md already documents the simulator test com…
  - Fix: Add a `test` step to ios-build.yml (or a sibling job on the xcode26 Mac runner): `xcodebuild test -project 'ios/Socratic Trade.xcodeproj' -scheme SocraticTrade -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=<available iPhone>' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO -resultBundlePath …`, discovering the simulator name from `xcrun simctl list`.

- **[ios-engineering:ios-10] PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy still absent although the app uses UserDefaults (required-reason API) — ITMS-91053 on upload / future App Store rejection** — `ios/project.yml:39` · defect · iOS · effort S · PARTIAL — True on main (`find ios -name '*.xcprivacy'` -> none; UserDefaults used in MobileStore.swift:139/144, CoachView.swift:10, MobileControlView.swift:96). Already addressed by in-flight PR #2794 (cursor/ios-release-readiness-2560-b532: adds ios/SocraticTrade/Priv… · prior 2026-08-06 H #2560 (still open)
  - Why: Apple has required a privacy manifest declaring `NSPrivacyAccessedAPICategoryUserDefaults` (reason CA92.1) since 2024; uploads warn today and are slated to be rejected. Flagged 2026-08-06 (#2560) and still open — ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption from the same batch was fixed, this half was not.
  - Fix: Add `ios/SocraticTrade/PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` (NSPrivacyAccessedAPITypes: UserDefaults CA92.1; NSPrivacyCollectedDataTypes: email/financial info, linked, not tracking; NSPrivacyTracking false) and ensure XcodeGen picks it up as a resource; verify with `xcodebuild -exportArchive` output.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-02] Coach replies render markdown as raw text (`**bold**`, `## headings`, `- bullets`, tables) — web renders GFM** — `ios/SocraticTrade/CoachView.swift:272` · bug · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: `Text(String)` is verbatim in SwiftUI (only string literals parse markdown), so the same assistant answer that reads cleanly on the web shows literal asterisks, pound signs and pipe tables on the phone. Coach is the owner's 'ask the desk' surface; unreadable replies undercut it.
  - Fix: Render assistant turns with `Text(AttributedString(markdown: text, options: .init(interpretedSyntax: .full)))` (or a small block renderer for headings/lists/tables since AttributedString flattens block structure), keep user turns plain, and add `.textSelection(.enabled)` so answers can be copied.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-03] Coach 'Suggested Draft' cannot be staged from iOS, yet the copy tells the owner to approve it in Proposals** — `ios/SocraticTrade/CoachView.swift:323` · defect · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The web draft card has a Stage action that creates a proposal via /api/proposals/from-draft; iOS CoachDraftCard is display-only (no button, no client call — `grep from-draft ios/` is empty). The card's own copy implies the draft is already waiting in Proposals, so the owner will open Proposals, find nothing, and conclude the app lost it.
  - Fix: Add a 'Stage for Approval' button on CoachDraftCard that POSTs /api/proposals/from-draft (mobile-side wrapper in MobileAPIClient), shows the staged/deduped result, and bumps the Proposals badge via `store.load()`. Until then, change the copy to say the draft is not yet a proposal and must be staged on the web.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-04] Scan table header advertises three side-by-side columns (Score · Px · Chg) but every row stacks those values vertically — header and rows do not align** — `ios/SocraticTrade/ScanView.swift:158` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The header row is a fixed-width three-column HStack while ScanRow renders score/price/change as one trailing VStack, so the labels float over nothing and 'Px' can sit above a change percentage. It reads as a broken table on the one iOS screen that is explicitly 'the interactive scan table'.
  - Fix: Either drop ScanTableHeader and label values inline (e.g. '82 score · $184.20 · +1.3%'), or make ScanRow a real three-column HStack using the same fixed widths (and drop the fixed widths in favor of `Grid`/`LazyVGrid` so Dynamic Type does not truncate). Add sort control (score/change) via a toolbar Menu to match the web scan.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-05] Insights hardcodes 'vs SPY' / 'SPY' while the snapshot carries `benchmark.benchmarkSymbol` (Home/Results use it) — wrong label when the benchmark is ^GSPC or a sector index** — `ios/SocraticTrade/InsightsView.swift:74` · bug · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling (2026-08-12) prefers real index data (^GSPC, GICS sector indices) with ETF fallback, so the benchmark symbol is not always SPY. Insights would then attribute the comparison to the wrong instrument — misinformation on a P&L-adjacent card.
  - Fix: Use `benchmark.benchmarkSymbol` in both title and detail (as Home and Results already do); ideally share one BenchmarkLine view across the three screens.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-06] Activity tab is described as 'Everything the agent did, newest first' but shows only fills plus the phone's own commands — no strategy-run/decision journal, so a run_failed push lands on a screen that cannot show the failure** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileControlView.swift:64` · defect · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior in-flight PR #2804 (2026-08-17 audit) / #2841
  - Why: The web Activity page is the decision journal (runs, decisions, alert center). iOS Activity renders Today tiles, scheduler, 20 fills, and `recentCommands` (mobile commands only). After 'Run Once' on the phone the owner has no way to see the run outcome (no proposals? why? which model failed?) — the most common daily question. The 2026-08-17 audit (in-flight PR #2804) flagged t…
  - Fix: Short term: change the More detail to 'Fills and the actions you took on this phone.' Medium term: extend /api/mobile/snapshot with the last N strategy runs (status, outcome word, proposal count, failure reason) and render a 'Runs' section at the top of Activity that the run_failed/run_completed deep link scrolls to. Coordinate with in-flight #2841 rather than duplicating.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-07] Positive/warning tones use system Color.green/Color.orange — P&L values, pills, and metric tiles fail WCAG AA contrast on the light default theme; web already moved to darker brand pos/neg/warn hexes** — `ios/SocraticTrade/AppComponents.swift:13` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R12; 2026-08-06 #2561 (web twin)
  - Why: System green (#34C759) on white/grouped background is ~2.2:1 and system orange (#FF9500) ~2.0:1 — below the 3:1 large-text floor, and these are exactly the Open P&L / Realized P&L numbers and 'Stale' pills the owner glances at outdoors. The web console fixed the same class of light-theme chip contrast (2026-08-06 #2561) by adopting #157f4e/#d1332e/#9a6209; iOS still ships the…
  - Fix: Define AppPalette.positive/negative/warning as trait-aware UIColors mirroring the console tokens (#157f4e / #d1332e / #9a6209 light; the dark twins from console.css), keep soft backgrounds at 0.09-0.12 opacity, and add an XCTest asserting each light foreground vs `.systemGroupedBackground` ≥ 4.5:1.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-08] Switching to a non-paper account triggers a destructive-role 'Use Brokerage Account?' alert while paper switches instantly — paper-as-safe-default ceremony the owner has ruled out** — `ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:1049` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling: 'An account is an account… don't treat paper as a safe home base, and don't add are-you-sure-it's-real-money ceremony.' Selecting which account the desk points at is not an order; the web Connections page switches with one click for every environment. The asymmetric alert (destructive red button) re-imposes exactly the paternalism the rulings forbid, and adds a t…
  - Fix: Remove `confirmingLiveActivation`; call `activate()` for every environment. If any acknowledgement is wanted, make it symmetric and non-modal (haptic + 'Switched to X' toast).

- **[performance-accounting:perf-13] iOS drops benchmarkUnavailable / unverifiedFlows / subPeriods and always claims 'cash flows neutralized'** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileModels.swift:458` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: When the SPY feed is dead the web shows a first-class 'benchmark unavailable' state; iOS just hides the card. When flows were 'inferred — unverified' and excluded, or no flows were detected, iOS still asserts neutralization. Same number, less honest framing on the phone.
  - Fix: Decode benchmarkUnavailable and unverifiedFlows; render the unavailable reason; make the caption conditional on cashFlowAdjusted and mention unverified count.

- **[security-auth:sec-05] Mobile web-auth handoff mints a session code for ANY authenticated GET with an arbitrary code_challenge — a scheme-squatting app plus one tapped link steals a 30-day session** — `app/api/mobile/auth-redirect/route.ts:24` · risk · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The server never saw the challenge at flow start, so it cannot tell an app-initiated callback from a link an attacker crafted with THEIR challenge. Chain: victim is logged into socratictrade.com in Safari and has any app that registers the socratictrade:// scheme (DeepLink.swift:31-33 itself notes 'any app on the device can register the same scheme'); attacker sends https://so…
  - Fix: Bind the challenge at flow start: add POST /api/mobile/auth/start that stores {code_challenge, exp} in a short-lived HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax cookie (or server-side nonce) that auth-redirect must present and match; and/or move the callback to a universal link (ASWebAuthenticationSession .https(host:path:) on iOS 17.4+ with the AASA already served) so only the signed app can rece…

- **[ux-copy-editor:copy-12] iOS proposal cards show a red "Live" pill on every live proposal — the rule and the iOS Home hero both say no Live callouts** — `ios/SocraticTrade/ProposalsView.swift:366` · defect · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: FLEET-UI-COPY: prefer not saying Live; no Live dots/pills. The web equivalent already renders BROKER (ui/primitives.tsx:177 LiveTag) and the iOS Home hero deliberately shows only a paper badge, so the Proposals tab is the one surface still shouting Live in red on the primary money-path card.
  - Fix: Drop the execution-mode pill for live proposals (keep a muted "paper" pill only), or render "Broker" in accent color to match the web LiveTag; keep the typed-confirm sheet wording ("Confirm Order for AAPL") without "Live".

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-02] iOS Safari status/toolbar tint follows OS scheme, not the console's actual (default LIGHT) theme** — `app/console/layout.tsx:15` · defect · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling: console defaults to light regardless of OS (useConsoleTheme.ts:19 "Missing / unknown → light"), and the user can also pick dark while the OS is light. theme-color is what iOS Safari 15+ / Chrome Android paint behind the URL bar and toolbar, so an OS-dark owner gets a near-black browser chrome over a light page (and vice-versa after choosing Dark). Same mismatch f…
  - Fix: In useConsoleTheme (or a shell effect) write the resolved --con-bg into `<meta name="theme-color">` and set body background/color-scheme to match the console's data-theme; keep the static media-query pair only as SSR fallback.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-05] iOS Safari auto-zooms on focus: ⌘K search input is 15px and Coach model/reasoning pickers + custom-model field force 11px inline** — `app/console/console.css:1251` · defect · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The project already knows iOS zooms any focused control under 16px (globals.css:225-231, console.css:654-660), but two paths bypass that floor: the palette input is a more-specific unlayered selector at 15px, and the Coach selects/TextInput set fontSize inline (inline beats everything). Opening ⌘K or tapping the model picker on an iPhone zooms the whole page and leaves it zoom…
  - Fix: Add `.con-cmdk-search input` to the ≤640px 16px block; replace the inline fontSize on chat.tsx selects/input with a class that yields to the phone floor (or `text-[length:var(--con-fs-xs)] max-sm:text-[16px]`).

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-06] Three different hard-coded bottom offsets vs the real tab-bar height — save bar and toasts collide with the tab bar when the home-indicator inset appears** — `app/console/components/policy-form.tsx:358` · defect · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The bar is ~59px of content (9+28+2+15+4 padding/icon/gap/label + 1px border) plus env(safe-area-inset-bottom), which on iOS Safari becomes non-zero as soon as the toolbar collapses on scroll. `bottom-16` (64px, z-40 < bar z-50) puts the Guardrails "Review & save" bar partly under the tab bar; toasts at 76px overlap tab labels; .ac-actions at 3.5rem sits ~3px under the bar eve…
  - Fix: Publish the measured bar height as a CSS variable on .console-root (`--con-tabbar-h`) from MobileTabBar's ResizeObserver, and use `calc(var(--con-tabbar-h) + 8px)` for .ac-actions, .con-toasts and the policy save bar; give the save bar z-index above the bar or the same clearance rule.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-07] Bottom sheets / More sheet / palette do not lock or contain scroll — page behind scrolls on iOS while a modal is open** — `app/console/console.css:858` · defect · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Only .con-drawer-body sets `overscroll-behavior: contain` (console.css:975). Scrolling a bottom sheet past its end, or dragging on the 12dvh scrim above it, scroll-chains to the underlying page on iOS Safari; the fixed sheet stays put while content behind moves and Safari's toolbar shows/hides, shifting env() insets under the sheet. On money sheets (STOP, Approve live typed-co…
  - Fix: Add `overscroll-behavior: contain` to .con-sheet, .con-cmdk-list and the TabsSheet scroller, and lock body scroll while any modal is open (set `overflow:hidden` on html/body or `position:fixed; top:-scrollY` pattern, restored on close) via a tiny useScrollLock hook shared by Sheet/TabsSheet/SymbolDrawer/CommandPalette.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-08] Full-screen overlays (symbol drawer, sheets, More sheet) ignore browser history — Android back / iOS edge-swipe leaves the console instead of closing the overlay** — `app/console/ui/symbol-drawer.tsx:63` · risk · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: On phones the drawer covers the entire viewport and reads as a new screen; the reflexive dismiss is the back gesture. Today that navigates away from /console/scan or /console/approvals (losing scroll/filter state), and the drawer/sheet reopens on forward. Escape (the only non-tap close) does not exist on a phone keyboard.
  - Fix: On open, `history.pushState({overlay:id})`; close on `popstate`; on programmatic close, `history.back()` if our state is on top. Wrap in the same hook as the scroll lock (mweb-07). Also make the drawer header sticky-safe: it already is (flex column), fine.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-09] Bottom-anchored sheets with typed-confirm inputs vs the on-screen keyboard — no interactiveWidget or visualViewport handling** — `app/console/layout.tsx:9` · defect · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Chrome Android ≥108 defaults to resizes-visual, and iOS never resizes the layout viewport, so a `position:fixed; bottom:0` sheet stays anchored to the layout-viewport bottom while the keyboard covers its lower ~40%: the typed-confirm field is auto-scrolled into view but the "Place broker order" / Cancel buttons (approval-card.tsx:1109-1120) can end up beneath the keyboard, and…
  - Fix: Add `interactiveWidget: "resizes-content"` to the console viewport export (fixes Android), and on iOS listen to `visualViewport` resize/scroll to set the sheet's `bottom`/`max-height` from `visualViewport.height` (or render sheets `top:0`-anchored when an input inside is focused). Keep the primary button visible above the keyboard.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-10] Toggle switch is a 36x20 target with no coarse-pointer bump and no clickable label** — `app/console/console.css:671` · defect · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Every other pressable primitive got the `@media (pointer: coarse)` 44px floor (console.css:458-465, 538-542) but the switch did not, and its label text is not associated, so on a phone the only hit area for e.g. "Auto-resume on boot", the typed-confirm switch, and data-source toggles is 20px tall — a mis-tap on a boot-safety switch is a correctness issue.
  - Fix: Add `.con-toggle` to the coarse-pointer block via a transparent ::before hit-area (min 44x44 without changing visuals), and give Toggle an optional `id` so callers wrap the row in `<label htmlFor>` (or make the whole row clickable).

- **[web-accessibility:a11y-07] ~147 explanations live only in native title= on non-interactive elements; Tooltip triggers on Chip/Stat/Ago are not focusable nor aria-described** — `app/console/ui/primitives.tsx:485` · defect · Mobile web · effort M · PARTIAL — Core claim confirmed: Tooltip (primitives.tsx:483-510) sets no tabIndex on the wrapping span for non-interactive children and doesn't wire aria-describedby. Counts are roughly right (I independently found 148 title= on div/span/td/th/p, 66 Chip-title occurren… · prior 2026-08-06 #2561 (3) — still open on main
  - Why: rg counts 147 title= on div/span/td/th/p in app/console (e.g. scan-table.tsx:463 cell titles, chrome.tsx:1044 error text behind 'delayed', positions.tsx:89-95 column definitions) plus 66 <Chip title>. On the owner's phone browser (a primary client) and for keyboard users none of that is reachable; the Tooltip wrapper has no tabIndex for non-interactive children and never links…
  - Fix: In Tooltip: add tabIndex={0} + role="button"/aria-expanded when the child is not interactive, wire useId + aria-describedby to the tooltip node, and keep it mounted while focused. Replace load-bearing td/span titles (freshness error, scan cells) with tap-to-open Tooltip or an inline detail line; keep native title only as a redundant desktop hint.

- **[public-site-growth:dpub-03] Public explainer copy promises delegation controls that do not exist in the product (“Evidence threshold: minimum source count, contradiction tolerance, freshness window”; “Review mode: notify before/after action, alert only when blocked”)** — `app/how-it-works/page.tsx:94` · defect · Public site · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The “Authority and overrides” section is written as what the owner can configure (“The interface separates confidence from permission… delegated scope is”), but neither an evidence-threshold knob nor a before/after review-mode selector exists in TradingPolicy or the Guardrails UI (authority is `propose | decide`, types.ts:24). On a marketing page for a trading product, describ…

Delete the two invented bullets or move them to a clearly labeled “planned” list.

  - Why: `applySocraticOverrideSizing` lets an LLM-authored `autonomyOverride.cashDeploymentPct` raise the sized notional (capped only by `socraticOverrideMaxPctOfNav`, which is `Infinity` when unset, and downstream policy caps) and it runs regardless of `socraticOverrideMode`. The owner chose this (2026-07-03 autonomy rollout), which is fine — but the public framework page, whose whol…

Mirror in docs/trading-framework.md. Optionally gate `applySocraticOverrideSizing` on `socr…

The public pages predate the helper and mix single-spaced prose with double-ASCII spaces that render as one; the legal pages (the longest prose on the site) are entirely single-spaced.
