Add a vitest that renders each public page and asserts no `[.!?] [A-Z]` single-space b…

### Could Be Improved (38)

- **[api-contract-web-ios:api-11] zod is a dependency but unused by all 122 routes; four different error envelopes plus plain-text 401/403 from middleware** — `middleware.ts:416` · improvement · API · effort L · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Clients must guess: iOS serverMessage tries `message` then `error` then raw text (MobileAPIClient.swift:437-451); browser code calling res.json() on a text 401 throws. Bodies are parsed with `request.json().catch(() => ({}))` and hand-rolled type checks (mobile/commands/route.ts:30-35, chat/route.ts:83-89), so limits like GET /api/mobile/commands?limit=abc become NaN → SQL LIM…
  - Fix: Introduce one `apiError(status, code, message)` helper returning `{ ok:false, error: code, message }` and use it in middleware and routes; adopt zod schemas at least for /api/mobile/commands payloads, /api/policy PATCH and /api/chat (the dependency is already there).

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-17] No invariant test that every API route resolves the request user or is explicitly public** — `test/route-ownership.test.ts:18` · improvement · API · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Owner ruling: keep security isolation. Today a new route that forgets `resolveRequestUserId` is caught only by middleware, and nothing lists which routes are intentionally public, so the allowlist drifts silently.
  - Fix: Add test/api-route-auth-invariant.test.ts that enumerates app/api/**/route.ts and asserts each file either references resolveRequestUser/resolveRequestUserId/requireAdmin or is in an explicit PUBLIC_ROUTES list with a one-line reason.

- **[backend-reliability:berel-06] No event-loop lag instrumentation anywhere — every loop-pin incident was reconstructed from broker latency and Uptime Robot; retries under a pin (awaitWithFirstCallRetry) just double broker load** — `src/lib/inflight-deadline.ts:128` · improvement · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The last week of receipts (alpaca-broker avg 3 s / max 14 s, ftsMirrorSlice 6-13 s, /api/health 4.2 s) are all symptoms of the same variable — event-loop delay — which is never measured. timeSync only covers hand-picked sync calls. Because logApiHealth latency includes queueing behind a pinned loop, broker 'slowness' is misattributed to Alpaca and the 16 s first-wait + second…
  - Fix: In instrumentation.ts start `perf_hooks.monitorEventLoopDelay({resolution: 20})`, expose p50/p95/p99 + max on /api/health and the ops snapshot, log `[slow-loop]` when p99 > 500 ms, and count it in journalLane records. Make the first-call retry conditional on loop p99 being low (a slow loop means 'wait', not 'retry'). Feed the number to the admin Operations panel so pins are se…

- **[backend-reliability:berel-07] All CPU-heavy RAG work (HTML extraction, chunking, FTS5 writes, JSON.parse of MB artifacts) runs on the single serving+trading event loop; no worker_threads or sidecar exists** — `src/lib/rag/sec-ingest-worker.ts:463` · opportunity · Backend · effort L · CONFIRMED · prior in-flight PR #2807 F4
  - Why: Four rounds of bounds (40-row groups, 250 ms adaptive stretch, 20/6 s slices, 6/2 s slices, pause-on-strategy) have each traded ingest throughput for a still-nonzero pin on the same loop that places orders and answers Docker health. Yielding cannot make a 300 ms synchronous SQLite call fair to HTTP. In-flight PR #2807 (F4) already names a sidecar/worker as the durable fix; not…
  - Fix: Move parse+chunk+FTS mirror into a `worker_threads` Worker that opens its own better-sqlite3 connection (WAL allows a second writer with busy_timeout) and receives {taskId, artifacts} messages; the main thread only claims tasks, heartbeats the lease and advances checkpoints. Land berel-01 first so the worker's per-row cost is O(1); then the SEC_INGEST_WORKER_ENABLED knob can s…

- **[backend-reliability:berel-09] Alpaca getEquityOrders walks the ENTIRE order history (status:all, up to 50×500) on every call, and it is called every 60 s by the stale-limit lane and again by pending-fill reconcile and the dashboard** — `src/lib/alpaca.ts:491` · improvement · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: scheduler.ts:884 (`gw.getEquityOrders` in stale-limit-scan) and strategy-execution.ts:1543 fetch it per tick; an autonomy account with a few thousand historical orders pays N sequential REST round trips per minute, so broker latency and 200 req/min budget degrade linearly with account age — plausibly part of the 6-14 s alpaca-broker latencies now blamed on the loop.
  - Fix: Add an `after` window (e.g., 30 days, or the oldest still-pending fill/proposal timestamp) to the default walk, expose a separate `getOpenEquityOrders` for the stale-exit and reconcile lanes, and memoize the per-tick result across lanes for the same account. Keep the unbounded walk only for the explicit history/backfill path.

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-07] Every Red Team review re-sends the ENTIRE Green user content (all candidates, RAG, learned, reflection, scorecards) — RedTeamReviewContext documents a subset the code ignores** — `src/lib/strategy.ts:6318` · improvement · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 D5
  - Why: red-team.ts:77-94 declares a curated context (macro, limits, portfolio, scorecards, analogs, candidatesUnderReview) and the approval-time re-review builds exactly that (strategy-execution.ts:580-593), but the run-time review spreads the full 48k-char evidence budget + full marketScan.topCandidates + retrievedFinancialContext + learnedContext + reflectionSummary, per opening, ×…
  - Fix: Project adversaryContext onto the interface keys + candidatesUnderReview + only the RAG snippets that mention the reviewed symbol; extend the Red data-not-command clause to whatever untrusted blocks remain. Expect a 3-5x Red-lane cost cut.

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-16] OpenRouter's billed `usage.cost` is never requested or stored; all cost figures come from a hand-maintained price table** — `src/lib/llm-usage.ts:180` · improvement · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-07-20 grok #18
  - Why: Every strategy/Red/tuning call goes through OpenRouter (llm-provider.ts:295-310) and OpenRouter returns exact billed credits when the body includes usage:{include:true}; instead estimateLlmCostUsd prices from MODEL_PRICE_PER_M (:59) plus a $15/M default for unknown ids, so /console/usage, the daily budget ceiling and usage-budget downgrades act on estimates that drift from the…
  - Fix: Add usage:{include:true} in buildLlmRequestBody for provider openrouter, persist usage.cost as cost_usd when present (fall back to the table otherwise) and flag rows as 'billed' vs 'estimated' on the Usage page.

- **[ops-observability-admin:sre-12] Broker-held protective stop declined/failed is audit-only; no owner push** — `src/lib/broker-protective-stops.ts:1542` · improvement · Backend · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 C4/C11
  - Why: All three failure branches (1542, 1551, 1588) write an audit row and queue a retry marker but call no sendNotification; the synthetic lane only pages after its own persistent failure (`protective_exit_failing`, synthetic-stops.ts:121). A trader who chose broker-held stops learns a stop is not resting only by scrolling Activity, and the 2026-08-06 review found positions with no…
  - Fix: Coalesce broker_protective_stop_error into the existing `protective_exit_failing` event (per symbol+fingerprint, one alert per streak, with 'synthetic fallback armed: yes/no' in the body) and show a 'Protection' badge on the position row when neither broker nor synthetic coverage is resting.

- **[performance-accounting:perf-14] Broker-authoritative equity history and cash-activity ledger are already implemented but unwired — inference could be replaced by facts** — `src/lib/alpaca-account-insights.ts:167` · opportunity · Backend · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Alpaca /v2/account/activities gives real CSD/CSW/ACATS transfers, DIV and FEE rows; /portfolio/history gives daily EOD equity and profit_loss; account.last_equity is the true prior-close baseline. Using them removes the entire class of phantom-flow bugs (perf-02/03, C1), fixes the 7-day window (perf-04), and gives a dividend/fee-aware P&L. The 'built-but-unwired rigor' theme f…
  - Fix: Per connected account: use activities (TRANS/CSD/CSW) as the flow ledger when available (fall back to inference otherwise, and label which), use portfolio/history for the daily equity curve, and last_equity for Day P&L. Needs per-account credential plumbing (resolvePrivateAlpacaAccount currently picks a ranked account).

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-10] maxWorkers:1 serializes ~7,000 tests: `npm test` is ~14 min of every CI run** — `vitest.config.ts:31` · improvement · Backend · effort S · PARTIAL — Core technical claim confirmed: vitest.config.ts:31 area has maxWorkers:1, testTimeout:60_000; land.sh sets VITEST_MAX_THREADS which vitest.config.ts never reads (grepped — zero references), so it is genuinely inert. However the '#1493 reduced from 4' attribu…
  - Why: With forks isolation each file already gets its own process, so the flakiness that motivated 1 worker was real network + import cost (both since fixed per the 2026-07-05 note). Every PR pays ~10 extra minutes and land.sh locally too; the inert env var in land.sh gives a false sense of parallelism.
  - Fix: After qa-05's network guard lands, set maxWorkers to 3 (ubuntu-latest has 4 vCPU) and lower testTimeout to 20s with per-file overrides for the known slow importers; replace `VITEST_MAX_THREADS` in land.sh with `--maxWorkers`. Track a flake list by file rather than throttling the whole suite.

- **[api-contract-web-ios:api-05] proposal.approve / proposal.reject are queued FIFO behind a running strategy.run_once (up to 30 min) with no ETA, while the web approve path is synchronous** — `src/lib/mobile-api.ts:88` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: An approve tapped on the phone during a run sits 'queued' until the run drains; the phone shows 'pending' with no queue position, and when it finally executes the price may have drifted enough that executeProposal returns 'proposed' (which api-02 then paints green). proposal.reject is a pure DB write and has no reason to wait. The console's approve returns 'busy' within a boun…
  - Fix: Make proposal.reject immediate; run proposal.approve immediately with the existing mutation-lease bounded wait so the phone gets 'busy'/'placed'/'blocked' in the same request (or return queue position + oldest-running startedAt in readiness.commandBacklog so the UI can say 'behind a run started 12m ago').

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-03] Dashboard snapshot is a 'load the universe' payload polled every 15 s: unused raw `audit[]` (incl. unbounded market_scan payloads), full 500-symbol `latestScan.quotesBySymbol`, full order history, duplicated profiles** — `src/lib/dashboard.ts:1108` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-07-20 #60
  - Why: `snapshot.audit` (100 raw audit rows with full payloads) has zero consumers in app/console, app/admin, or ios/ (grep) — the client uses `auditFeed`/`unifiedFeed`. When a `market_scan` audit is among the last 100 rows its whole scan (~600 KB avg, 2.7 MB p90 per the 2026-08-02 audit investigation) is sent as `audit[]` payload AND again as `latestScan`. `latestScan.quotesBySymbol…
  - Fix: Drop `audit` from the response (keep `auditFeed`/`unifiedFeed`); trim `latestScan` to `topCandidates` + `quotesBySymbol` filtered to symbols referenced by positions/orders/pending proposals (the client fallbacks in orders/page.tsx:278 and derive.ts only key by those); send `activeProfileId` instead of a second copy of `activeProfile`. Longer term split the snapshot into ETag'd…

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-09] Sentry Session Replay bundle (~38 KB gz) ships on every page although replay is env-disabled; Sentry core is ~160 KB gz of every console load** — `instrumentation-client.ts:31` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Statically referencing `Sentry.replayIntegration` keeps the replay module in the graph; the production build has chunk c9eecfba (120,992 B raw / 38,094 B gz, 68 `replay` refs) plus 8863 (509,252 B raw / 159,661 B gz Sentry core) loaded by every route in the approvals/scan/settings chunk sets. That is ~200 KB gz — about 40% of a console page's JS — for a feature that is off, an…
  - Fix: Use `Sentry.lazyLoadIntegration("replayIntegration")` inside the `replayEnabled` branch, and set `webpack.treeshake: { removeDebugLogging: true, excludeReplayIframe: true, excludeReplayShadowDom: true, excludeReplayWorker: true }` in next.config.mjs (options exist in @sentry/nextjs 10.70 types). Consider `removeTracing` if browser tracing is not used.

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-10] `motion` (framer) shared chunk (~40 KB gz / 124 KB raw) is loaded on every console page for one tooltip fade** — `app/console/ui/primitives.tsx:7` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: primitives.tsx is imported by every console page, so chunk 9249 (124,371 B raw / 40,728 B gz, 53 `projection`/18 `layoutId` refs = full motion runtime) is in the critical JS of every route for a 150 ms opacity/translate on the Tooltip. That is roughly the size of the whole approvals page chunk.
  - Fix: Replace with a CSS transition (`transition: opacity .15s, transform .15s` + `@starting-style` or a data-open class) or, if exit animation matters, `LazyMotion features={domAnimation}` + `m.div` to load ~1/3 of the runtime lazily. Remove `motion` from dependencies if nothing else needs it.

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-11] HTML -> JS -> /api/dashboard is a strict waterfall: the (up to ~24 s) snapshot request cannot start until ~1.5 MB raw JS is parsed and hydrated** — `app/console/layout.tsx:22` · opportunity · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Every console route is client-only behind the loader, so the first meaningful data request starts after download+parse of ~476 KB gz JS (1.47 MB raw for /console/approvals) and React mount — on a phone that is ~0.5-1 s of pure dead time added in front of a broker chain that is already the slowest thing in the product. The layout is a server component and could emit the hint fo…
  - Fix: In app/console/layout.tsx render `<link rel="preload" as="fetch" href="/api/dashboard" crossorigin="use-credentials">` (or an inline `<script>` that starts `fetch("/api/dashboard")` and stashes the promise on `window.__stSnapshot` for useConsoleData to adopt), and open the EventSource in the same early script. Measure with the browser Performance panel: first `/api/dashboard`…

- **[ops-observability-admin:sre-10] Provider/storage alerts re-fire on fixed cooldowns with no 'recovered' event and no 'since/nth reminder', and storage alerting runs only as a side effect of GET /api/health** — `src/lib/db-health.ts:574` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 D3 (mutes landed; recovery/closure not)
  - Why: alertConnectionFailure and alertStorageWarning (db-health.ts:775-843) send the same title every 6h/12h while a condition persists and never send closure (rg 'recover' in db-health.ts finds only a comment), so on the phone the owner cannot tell 'still broken since Monday' from 'broke again' and never learns it healed. All litestream/tier alerts are evaluated exclusively inside…
  - Fix: Track first-seen per alert key and include 'since <CT time> · reminder N' in the body; emit a one-shot '<service> recovered' / 'Backups healthy again' when the condition clears (clear the cooldown key on success). Move the storage/tier evaluation into a scheduler lane (every 5 min) with the same alertStorageWarning keys; keep /api/health read-only.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-10] Approve is starved for the entire duration of an autonomous run (which can be 15-20 min per STATUS.md) because executeProposal contends for the whole-run strategy lock; console gives up after ~2 min, iOS shows 'Approved' (tsx-03)** — `src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:297` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The lock exists for a daily-cap TOCTOU that the owner has ruled advisory anyway, and the placement window is already serialized by withAccountMutation. Risk-reducing exit approvals (Risk-Exit sells) are refused while an LLM cycle owns the loop — the exact moment the owner most wants to act. app/console/lib/api.ts:306-309 retries busy for ~2 min then surfaces 'Approval is still…
  - Fix: Let executeProposal proceed on the mutation lease alone (re-reading dailyExecutionStats inside the lease), or at least never block sell/cover approvals on the run lock; keep the run lock only for run-vs-run exclusivity.

- **[trading-money-path:tsx-12] 'Not placed — safe to retry' has no retry path: not_placed is terminal and executeProposal refuses anything but status=proposed** — `src/lib/dashboard-ui.ts:326` · improvement · Cross-surface · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The lease-lost / broker-reachable-but-absent branches (strategy-execution.ts:1332,1388; strategy.ts:4011,4074) tell the owner it is safe to retry, and app/console/lib/thesis.ts:91 says 'can be reviewed and retried', but there is no button, endpoint, or status transition to re-queue the card. The owner must wait for the next run to re-propose (which may not) or hand-type a chat…
  - Fix: Add POST /api/proposals/[id]/requeue (CAS not_placed→proposed with a fresh refId, audit) and a Retry action on the console card + iOS; or auto-requeue once when the cause was lease-lost.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-03] Home hierarchy: no attention/equity hero, account stats mislabeled, duplicated cadence/spend blocks** — `app/console/page.tsx:276` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R6
  - Why: A daily trader's first glance has no 'what needs me now' (NeedsAttention in components/needs-attention.tsx is unmounted; pending count only lives in the rail badge) and no equity headline; Portfolio Value/Day P&L sit under a learning-loop title. State chip + last-run repeat in the strategy bar (146-174) and the 'Run cadence' card (419-451); daily spend appears three times (str…
  - Fix: Top row: equity + day P&L hero, pending-proposals CTA, run state/next run; rename the stats card 'Account'; delete the Run cadence card; single spend indicator.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-05] Decisions route is orphaned from nav; proposal lifecycle spread over five surfaces** — `app/console/decisions/page.tsx:1` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 H #2556
  - Why: Decision traces are the product's evidence spine yet /console/decisions has no rail item (nothing is highlighted while on it), is reachable only via Home 'All Decisions', and Home rows/Approval cards/Activity runs never deep-link into a trace. Meanwhile 'Proposals', 'Decisions', Activity→Runs, Home Latest run/Previous Trades and Results→By thesis all present the same lifecycle…
  - Fix: Add Decisions to the Review group (or fold the index into Activity→Runs), link every proposal/decision row to its trace, and publish one status→label→tone map used by all five surfaces.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-08] Positions blotter lives only on Home and lacks last price, day change, sort and any exit action** — `app/console/components/positions.tsx:89` · opportunity · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: There is no Portfolio/Positions destination — a trader must scroll Home to see holdings, and cannot sort by P&L/weight, see today's move per name, or act (the only exit path is asking Coach to draft, then Approvals). The from-draft→approve rail already exists (app/api/proposals/from-draft), so a per-row 'Draft exit' would keep the no-manual-order-entry design.
  - Fix: Add a Portfolio destination (Monitor group) with sortable columns incl. Last/Day chg/Day P&L, and a 'Draft exit…' row action that posts a from-draft proposal for the owner to approve.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-09] Three different save models across the Configure pages (and mixed within Guardrails)** — `app/console/guardrails/page.tsx:335` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-07-20 UI #6/#12
  - Why: Guardrails = draft + 'Review changes' sheet, Strategy = blur autosave, Settings = per-control autosave with SaveStatus; the Tax card on Guardrails autosaves while its neighbours need Save. The owner cannot predict whether an edit is live yet, which matters when a cap or model choice affects the next run.
  - Fix: Pick one model per page class (money-affecting policy → draft+Review; preferences → autosave) and label the pattern in the card header ('Saves on Review' / 'Saves instantly').

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-10] Activity journal and Orders history cannot be searched, filtered or exported; hard caps** — `app/console/activity/page.tsx:216` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: 'Decision journal: everything the agent did' offers four tabs but no symbol/date/status filter, no search, no load-more beyond 60/40/100 rows; Orders shows the last 20 finished orders with no date range and no CSV. A daily trader reviewing a week, a ticker, or tax lots has to leave the app.
  - Fix: Add symbol/date/status filters + load-more to Activity, a date-range + 'Export CSV' on Orders/Fills (reuse /api/history), and link rows to decision traces.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-11] Command palette lacks ticker lookup, STOP/Start, and account switch verbs** — `app/console/components/command-palette.tsx:160` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R7
  - Why: ⌘K is the desk's fastest surface but typing 'NVDA' does nothing (SymbolDrawer exists), and the money-critical verbs from the 2026-08-04 R7 backlog (STOP, Start, switch account) are missing while Run once is present — the one verb that spends. Settings hits landed (#2558) — good.
  - Fix: Add symbol search → open drawer, 'Stop agent'/'Start agent' → ControlSheet, 'Switch to <account>' rows, and 'Open proposal <SYM>'.

- **[desktop-web-ux:dweb-12] Multi-account desk: no cross-account overview; switching reloads the whole console** — `app/console/components/chrome.tsx:127` · opportunity · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: With Paper + Roth live (STATUS.md), the rail badge, Home, and Orders show the active account only; the only cross-account glance is the state chip per row inside the scope menu (163-167). A pending Roth proposal is invisible while Paper is loaded, and each switch is a full reload with intro splash.
  - Fix: Add an 'All accounts' strip on Home (equity, day P&L, pending, run state per account, click to switch) and make the rail badge total pending across accounts with a per-account tooltip.

- **[frontend-performance:dweb-06] Single console context re-renders every page 3x per poll and once per SSE event because `fetching` and `stream` health live in the same context value as `snapshot`** — `app/console/lib/useConsoleData.tsx:341` · improvement · Desktop web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: 40 call sites read `useConsoleData()`, including every page root and the shell; only FreshnessStrip/MobileFreshnessBar (shell.tsx:202,211) read `stream`, and `retrying` is only meaningful pre-snapshot. Each poll produces three distinct context values (fetching=true, new snapshot, fetching=false) and every SSE event (`ready`, `market-data`, `order`, `proposal`, `dirty` — market…
  - Fix: Split into two contexts (`ConsoleSnapshotContext` = {snapshot, fetchedAt, error, refresh}; `ConsoleStatusContext` = {stream, retrying, slowFirstLoad, loading}); keep `lastEventAt/lastEventType` in a ref and only publish `status` transitions; only flip `fetching` state while `snapshot === null`. Wrap the freshness strips in the status context.

- **[llm-agent-architecture:llm-15] /console/usage still shows no embedding/rerank spend — RAG cost is only on the admin RAG-coverage page** — `app/console/usage/page.tsx:10` · improvement · Desktop web · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-06 D4
  - Why: meterEmbed/meterRerank (vector-db.ts:6777, rag-metering.ts) book the 10-K backfill and rerank calls that drove the 'OpenRouter credits low' alerts, but the owner-facing Usage page only lists chat/strategy LLM contexts, so the spend class most likely to drain prepaid credits is invisible where the owner looks.
  - Fix: Add an 'Embeddings / RAG' context row (embed tokens, rerank calls, estimated $) to LlmUsageClient from getRagUsageSummary, per account and per day.

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-12] ESLint warn backlog has no ratchet: 49 console warnings (26 set-state-in-effect), 346 in src/api/admin** — `eslint.config.mjs:52` · improvement · Desktop web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Because CI only fails on errors, the count can grow unbounded; `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` in console pages is a real double-render/effect-cascade source on the phone-width site, and 162 unused vars is dead code the next agent will misread.
  - Fix: Add scripts/eslint-ratchet.mjs that stores the per-rule warning baseline in a JSON file and fails `verify` when a count rises; promote `@typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars` to error with `argsIgnorePattern: "^_"` after a one-time burn-down; burn the 26 console set-state-in-effect sites down first.

- **[web-accessibility:a11y-05] No skip link — ~22 focus stops (chrome bar + 15-item rail) precede <main> on every console page** — `app/console/components/shell.tsx:180` · improvement · Desktop web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: ScopeSelector, StateChip, Admin, Cmd+K, avatar, Run once, Stop Agent, then 15 rail links (nav.tsx:112-135) all sit before main content; there is no 'Skip to content' link anywhere (grep 'skip to' → none). WCAG 2.4.1 bypass blocks; a keyboard trader tabs through the whole chrome on every navigation.
  - Fix: Add a visually-hidden-until-focused <a href="#con-main" className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only …">Skip to content</a> as the first child of .console-root and id="con-main" tabIndex={-1} on <main>. Also move focus to the page h1 on client-side route change.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-09] First-run on iOS is a dead end: the checklist sends the owner to 'Account & Settings' to connect Alpaca/Robinhood, but that sheet can only select accounts; the in-flight Safari handoff loses the session — reuse the AdminPortalView cookie-sharing web view instead** — `ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:87` · improvement · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior in-flight PR #2794
  - Why: On an empty account the Home hero says 'Finish setup to trade' and points at a place that cannot connect a broker; universe copy (`:109`) points at 'the Strategy page' that does not exist on the phone. PR #2794 (open) adds 'Open Connections' / 'Open Strategy' links that open Safari — but Safari has no session cookie, so the owner must sign in again there. AdminPortalView alrea…
  - Fix: Generalize AdminPortalView into a `ConsoleWebSheet(path:)` fenced to /console/connections and /console/strategy, present it from the checklist CTA and the empty-accounts row, and call `store.load()` on dismiss so the new account/universe appears without a manual pull. Neutralize broker names in the copy ('Connect a broker account'). Coordinate with #2794 so the two do not both…

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-10] Positions rows show market value and average cost but no per-position P&L, last price, or day change — the trader has to open every sheet to see what is winning** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MarketsView.swift:140` · improvement · iOS · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: The unrealized P&L is already computable from the snapshot (PositionDetailCard does it) but the list hides it; a holdings list without P&L is the single most-missed glance on a phone. Rows also do not sort (biggest positions/losers first) and there is no per-position mark price.
  - Fix: Add a tinted P&L line (`$ and %`, pos/neg color) to PositionRow using the same formula, and a `sort` Menu (market value / P&L / symbol). Consider adding `lastPrice` and `dayChangePct` to the mobile snapshot's Position payload so the row can show '$184.20 · +1.3% today'.

- **[ios-hig-ux:ios-11] No success acknowledgement or haptics for money actions — Run Once, Approve, Cancel Order, Add to Watchlist, Create Alert just stop spinning** — `ios/SocraticTrade/AppComponents.swift:894` · improvement · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R10
  - Why: After tapping Run Once the owner sees the spinner clear and no confirmation that a run was queued; approve/reject show an on-card strip but watchlist/alert/cancel/policy edits show nothing on success (and errors are hidden per ios-01). HIG expects a haptic and a transient confirmation for consequential actions; the 2026-08-04 panel already asked for an iOS command-outcome bann…
  - Fix: Add `.sensoryFeedback(.success/.error, trigger:)` on CommandButton outcomes and a lightweight toast overlay in the tab shell (‘Run once queued · Roth’, ‘Order cancel sent’, ‘NVDA added to watchlist’) driven by a `store.notice` published value; reuse the same overlay for ios-01 errors.

- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-09] MobileStore.submit/load are untested despite an existing URLProtocol harness** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:358` · improvement · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: submit() is the path every approve/reject/stop/activate tap takes (idempotency key, busy dedupe, pendingAccountId, proposal feedback) and load() decides stale-snapshot fallback and 401 handling; none of that has a test, so a regression shows up as double-submitted commands or a blank Home on the phone.
  - Fix: Add ios/SocraticTradeTests/MobileStoreTests.swift using the existing MobileTestURLProtocol: (1) submit sends the Idempotency-Key header and refuses a second submit while busy; (2) submit with an unadvertised command sets `error` and does not hit the network; (3) load() on a 500 keeps the cached snapshot and sets snapshotLoadFailed; (4) load() on 401 after init routes through a…

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-11] No phone type-scale bump: 412 uses of 11px and 185 of 12.5px text render at desktop sizes on a 6-inch screen** — `app/console/console.css:90` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Body copy on proposals, hints, table cells and chips is overwhelmingly `sm`/`xs`; on a phone held at arm's length 11-12.5px is below iOS/Android body defaults (17pt/16sp) and forces pinch-zoom on the very screens the owner triages from a phone. Because everything is tokenized, this is a one-block fix with no per-component churn.
  - Fix: Add `@media (max-width: 640px) { .console-root { --con-fs-xs: 12px; --con-fs-sm: 14px; --con-fs-base: 15px; --con-fs-md: 16px; } }` (and let 2xs stay for captions), then spot-check the chrome bar/tab bar which use literal 10px.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-12] Phone chrome-bar controls are 32px (avatar, ⌘K trigger, run-state chip) — known follow-up still open** — `app/console/components/chrome.tsx:900` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 P2 (32px avatar)
  - Why: The three secondary bar controls sit at 32px next to a 44px Stop button; the StateChip (chrome.tsx:300-315, py-1 with 11px/10px stacked text) is ~30px tall and opens the money-relevant ControlSheet. The 32px avatar was already flagged (2026-08-04 P2 "Settings touch targets (32px avatar)") and remains.
  - Fix: Give these a `.con-bar-ctl` hook and include it in the `(pointer: coarse)` 44px block (visual size can stay 32 via inner element); fix the stale 44px comment.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-13] Overlay close/pin/row targets under 44px: sheet X is 18px, drawer X 32px, More-sheet pins 36px and rows ~35px, scan star 28px, expand toggle text-only** — `app/console/ui/sheet.tsx:157` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: On phones these are the primary ways out of a modal (no Escape key) and the primary way to pin tabs / watch a symbol / expand a proposal's full reasoning; an 18px X in the corner of a bottom sheet is easy to miss and mis-taps land on the header.
  - Fix: Standardize an `IconTarget` wrapper (44x44 hit area, 18px glyph) used by Sheet, TabsSheet, SymbolDrawer; add `@media (pointer:coarse){ .con-nav-item{min-height:44px} .ac-expand-toggle{min-height:44px} }` and bump WatchButton to h-9/w-9 with a coarse floor.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-14] Watchlist has no phone card variant — a 5-column table with row actions in the last column scrolls its Alert/Delete buttons off-screen** — `app/console/watchlist/page.tsx:227` · improvement · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Orders (orders/page.tsx:402), Positions (positions.tsx:158) and Scan (scan-table.tsx:505) all switch to stacked cards below lg; Watchlist does not, so at 360-430px the ghost `Alert`/trash buttons (watchlist/page.tsx:262-286) sit past the right edge with no scroll affordance and 28px-tall `size="sm"` targets. Watchlist is a natural phone screen (glance at prices, arm an alert).
  - Fix: Add the same `lg:hidden` card list (symbol + price + armed count + Alert/Remove buttons at ≥40px) and hide the table below lg; while at it add a subtle right-edge fade to `.overflow-x-auto` tables that still exist on Results.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-15] Home on a phone buries portfolio value / day P&L under the readiness hero, strategy bar and "Latest strategy run" list** — `app/console/page.tsx:217` · improvement · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R6
  - Why: On phone the grid collapses to one column so the first thing after the strategy bar is a list of last-run proposals; equity, day P&L, cash and next-run appear only after scrolling past them. A trader opening the site on a phone wants the number first. Prior review R6 ("Ready-state Home density: equity/P&L hero") is still open.
  - Fix: Below xl, render a compact 2x2 stat strip (Portfolio value, Day P&L, Cash/BP, Deployed today) directly under the strategy bar (reuse the existing Stat components), and collapse Coach/Framework cards by default on phones.

## 6. Surface-By-Surface Digest

### Desktop Web
- Home: dead Approve on latest-run rows + wrong tones (`dweb-01`), click-only rows (`a11y-01`, `dweb-18`), no attention/equity hero and duplicated cadence/spend blocks (`dweb-03`), Coach card with dead quick-action chips (`dweb-07`), positions blotter without last price / day change / exit action (`dweb-08`).
- Navigation and IA: Decisions route orphaned from nav while the proposal lifecycle is spread over five surfaces (`dweb-05`); default 404 inside the console (LIVE-02); command palette lacks ticker lookup / STOP / account switch (`dweb-11`); bare `r` fires a run (`dweb-02`); freshness strip only at document end (`dweb-04`).
- Configure pages: three save models across Strategy / Guardrails / Connections (`dweb-09`); Settings tab strip overflows with no affordance (LIVE-13); Guardrails header says "hard execution constraints" and the Autonomy card contradicts itself (LIVE-04); Proposals page refuses reject while stopped (LIVE-05).
- Accessibility: contrast failures on tone chips + Stop/Start (`a11y-02`), no skip link (`a11y-05`), same document title on every route (`a11y-04`), sheet Escape not stack-aware (`a11y-03`), toasts auto-dismiss without pause (`a11y-06`), ~147 explanations only in `title=` (`a11y-07`).
- Performance: Connections bundle 1.05 MB (`dweb-01` perf), dashboard snapshot is a "load the universe" payload every 15 s (`dweb-03` perf), 3× re-render per poll (`dweb-06` perf), Sentry replay + `motion` shipped everywhere (`dweb-09`, `dweb-10`), HTML→JS→/api/dashboard strict waterfall (`dweb-11` perf), TickerLogo requests dark then light (`dweb-07` perf).

### Mobile Web (phone-width site)
- Chrome bar over budget since "Stop Agent" (`mweb-01`, LIVE-11); iOS Safari tint follows OS not the light theme (`mweb-02`); auto-zoom on 11–15px inputs (`mweb-05`); hard-coded bottom offsets collide with the tab bar (`mweb-06`); sheets do not lock scroll (`mweb-07`) and ignore browser history (`mweb-08`); typed-confirm inputs vs the keyboard (`mweb-09`); 36×20 toggles (`mweb-10`); no phone type-scale bump (`mweb-11`); many sub-44px targets (`mweb-12`, `mweb-13`); Watchlist has no card variant (`mweb-14`); Home buries portfolio value / day P&L (`mweb-15`); "System" theme lost on reload (`mweb-03`); ⌘K theme toggle inert (`mweb-04`); "#autonomy" literal (LIVE-12).

### iOS
- Correctness: sign-out leaves cached snapshot (`ios-engineering:ios-01`); silent errors in Settings sheet (`ios-hig-ux:ios-01`); approve "succeeded" on blocked (`api-02`/`tsx-03`); stop-loss "—" (`api-01`); scan timeout below server budget (`api-03`); 3-minute staleness gate (`ios-engineering:ios-03`); cold-start push tap dropped (`ios-05`); all-or-nothing decode (`api-07`); iOS drops `benchmarkUnavailable`/`unverifiedFlows` and always claims cash flows neutralized (`perf-13`); Insights hardcodes SPY (`ios-hig-ux:ios-05`); "1 Proposals" (LIVE-15).
- Product / HIG: Coach markdown raw (`ios-02`); Suggested Draft cannot be staged (`ios-03`); Scan header vs stacked rows (`ios-04`); Activity is fills + own commands only (`ios-06`); system green/orange fail contrast (`ios-07`); destructive-role alert on brokerage switch (`ios-08` — owner-philosophy); first-run dead end (`ios-09`); positions without P&L (`ios-10`, LIVE-14); no success acknowledgement / haptics for money actions (`ios-11`); push permission prompt without pre-prompt (LIVE-16); loosening rituals (LIVE-17 — owner decision); tab/parity mismatch with web and no Strategy/Connections/Settings on the phone (LIVE-18); "Live" pill on every live proposal (`copy-12`); universe copy points at a non-existent Strategy page (`copy-04`).
- Engineering / ship: Swift tests never run in CI or before TestFlight (`qa-01`, `ios-09` eng), Swift build not a merge gate (`qa-02`), `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` missing (`ios-10` eng), MobileStore submit/load untested (`qa-09`), verifier-added: `.serverError` releases the command attempt on 502/503/504 (possible double submit) and a single failed background reload trips `snapshotLoadFailed`.

### Public Site
- Over-promised controls (`dpub-03`), untrue `/framework` invariant (`dpub-04`), Terms not updated for data pooling (`dpub-05`), `/access-denied` dead end for every Auth.js error (`dpub-02`), deep-link lost on session expiry (`dpub-01`), no two-space gaps anywhere (`dpub-06`), sentence-case CTAs "Request access" / "Decision framework" (LIVE), no mention of the iOS app on any growth page (verifier-added), login page prints env-var names when auth is unconfigured (LIVE).

### API + Backend
- Money path: `tsx-01`, `tsx-02`, `tsx-04`, `tsx-05`, `tsx-06` (tradability stub), `tsx-08`, `tsx-09`, `tsx-10` (approve starved by autonomous run), `tsx-11` (hard-coded 5% headroom gate with no policy toggle — owner-philosophy), `tsx-12` ("safe to retry" with no retry path), `tsx-14` (remediation-deferred spam).
- Accounting: `perf-01`…`perf-14` (see §1 item 4; plus HWM not flow-adjusted / UTC day `perf-08`, first-partial-exit grading `perf-09`, 20 bps haircut on real Alpaca-paper fills `perf-10`, closing fills without app lot dropped `perf-12`, broker-authoritative equity/cash ledger implemented but unwired `perf-14`).
- Reliability: `berel-01`…`berel-10` (FTS full scan, sync FTS mirrors, no broker timeout, no tick re-entrancy guard, no event-loop-lag instrumentation, all CPU-heavy RAG on the serving thread, resume slices re-parse artifacts).
- LLM: `llm-01`…`llm-16` (strict schema, wrong account in Red Team, macro pruning, auto-applied directives, half-approve without haircut, full Green content re-sent to Red, json_object transports off-schema, dead-model list, late responses unmetered, native-provider Coach routing, `MockLLM` semantic gate in production, no `usage.cost` capture).
- Data: `mdi-01`…`mdi-13` (Friday freeze, 3-day bars, fabricated asOf ×2, ask-as-price, VWAP yesterday, screener asOf sentence, preferreds/units in dynamic universes, mixed volume semantics, no early-close calendar, unlabeled adjustment mixing, Yahoo v7 crumb, UTC day keys).
- API contract: `api-01`…`api-11` (nested `riskRules`, approve success lie, scan timeout, full StrategyResult stored in `mobile_commands`, FIFO behind run_once, no generated fixtures, all-or-nothing decode, zod unused / four error envelopes).
- Security: `sec-01`…`sec-06`.  Ops: `sre-01`…`sre-14`.  QA: `qa-01`…`qa-17`.

### Cross-Surface Parity
- One run state with three names and one stop control with four (`copy-07`); "Approvals" vs "Proposals" (`copy-05`); "Realized/Unrealized P&L" mean three different things on Results, Home and iOS (`perf-05`); win rate "0%" for zero closed lots on web + iOS (`perf-06`); overdue next run never flagged on either surface (`sre-11`); tabs differ web-mobile vs iOS (LIVE-18); Run once not stamped with account + LIVE|PAPER on either (2026-08-04 R1, still open).

## 9. Strengths — Do Not Churn

Deduped lightly; each lane's own words, prefixed with the lane.

- [ios-hig-ux] Two-space sentence rule is applied consistently across every user-facing Swift string (a grep for single-space sentence gaps in string literals returns only 'Avg. Return') — do not churn.
- [ios-hig-ux] One deep-link router (DeepLink.destination(for:)) shared by universal links and tapped pushes, with https-only + host allow-list and proposal-id validation; PushPayload only extracts and never routes — clean and testable.
- [ios-hig-ux] SnapshotScaffold gives every tab the same loading / empty / stale / pull-to-refresh / TimelineView freshness contract, and MobileStore gates commands on the server-advertised control catalog while never gating protective commands (Stop / Close Only / Wind Down) — good correctness-harde…
- [ios-hig-ux] Owner-customizable tab bar mirrors the web mobile tabs (min 2 / max 4, canonical order, More always reachable, deep links reroute into the More stack) using the iOS 26 `Tab` builder — a genuinely native tab model.
- [ios-hig-ux] Honest data conventions carried to iOS: P/E 'n/a' vs '—', Red Team failure kinds mirrored from red-team-routing, benchmark 'cash flows neutralized' caption, realized P&L shown as '—' when there is no fill ledger, and no fabricated numbers anywhere.
- [ios-hig-ux] Typography uses `Font.custom(_:size:relativeTo:)` so Dynamic Type scales the whole app, and icon-only controls carry accessibilityLabel/hint (watchlist remove, cancel order, pin toggles, tab customization hints); APNs environment is read from the embedded provisioning profile rather th…
- [ios-engineering] Web sign-in handoff is genuinely well designed: PKCE-bound one-time code in server memory (src/lib/mobile-auth-handoff.ts), the session JWT never crosses the custom URL scheme, `socratictrade://` is rejected as a content route in DeepLink.swift, and `resolvedURL` pins https + host…
- [ios-engineering] APNs environment is resolved from the embedded provisioning profile's `aps-environment`, not `#if DEBUG`, with a pure decision table and tests (PushNotifications.swift:29-90) — this is the failure mode most apps ship; keep it.
- [ios-engineering] Command submission is idempotent end to end: `CommandAttemptTracker` + `Idempotency-Key: <type>:<uuid>` header, key retained on network/decoding errors and released on server errors, with reconciliation against `recentCommands` (MobileStore.swift:4-71, 369-457).
- [ios-engineering] Deep-link routing is single-sourced and contract-tested against the server (DeepLink.swift ↔ src/lib/push-deep-links.ts ↔ test/apns-deep-link-contract.test.ts + PushNotificationTests) and pure/total — every unknown input maps to nil.
- [ios-engineering] Error copy discipline: Cloudflare 52x mapped to plain language, Apple/APNs jargon scrubbed from settings copy, two-space sentences honored, and protective commands (stop/close-only/wind-down/reject/cancel) explicitly exempt from freshness/catalog gates — matches the owner's adviso…
- [ios-engineering] Snapshot decoding tolerates missing sections and a malformed `catalog` (try? + built-in fallback), `RedTeamVerdict` accepts legacy string and object shapes, and OrderCancel mirrors the server's working-state predicate verbatim with rationale — good API-drift posture at the section…
- [desktop-web-ux] Chrome control model is right and well-worded: single Run once, Start/Stop Agent, STOP-never-sells copy in ControlSheet, typed WIND DOWN for the only verb that sells (chrome.tsx 352-523).
- [desktop-web-ux] Approval card + bulk approve: collapsed receipt, 'Approve live' + BROKER tag, paste-blocked typed batch sheet, per-row re-check messaging, 20-per-batch rate guard (approvals/page.tsx, approval-card.tsx).
- [desktop-web-ux] Guardrails page: Essentials / Protective stops with StopFlowDiagram / Advanced rulebook collapsed by default, cap-utilization meters beside the caps, advisory framing — best-in-product configure surface.
- [desktop-web-ux] Results honesty: TWR vs SPY with 'benchmark unavailable' state and phantom-transfer exclusion, gated Red Team efficacy and Signal-health/Lookahead audits with plain tooltips (results/page.tsx 230-345).

### Related Report-Only Audits (Cursor, 2026-08-17 — read alongside this)

Cursor landed a parallel set of report-only audits the day before this review; overlap is expected and intentional (two independent readers).  Merged on `main`: `docs/audits/2026-08-17-blind-spots.md` (#2810 — legal/regulatory F-LEG-1..7, product-strategy, a11y F-A11Y-1..3, i18n, DX F-DX-1..3), `2026-08-17-purchases-stripe-storekit.md` (#2809), `2026-08-17-cross-app-coordination.md`, `2026-08-18-pinecone-store-vs-condense.md` (#2811).  Open PRs at time of writing: #2803 (RAG ingest/retrieval/learning), #2804 (web / mobile-web / iOS parity), #2805 (brokers + data-cascade reliability), #2806 (security + reliability), #2807 (architecture + backend), #2808 (trading-outcomes validation).  Where this review and those audits agree (e.g. blind-spots F-A11Y-2 "no skip link" = `a11y-05`; F-DX-2 "required CI never runs Playwright or Swift tests" = `qa-01`/`qa-02`/`qa-06`; F-A11Y-3 iOS UIKit chrome ignores Dynamic Type), treat that as two-reader confirmation.

## 11. Suggested Sequencing

Three tranches, sized for a solo owner + agents.  Ids are the work items; effort is the panel's S/M/L.

### Tranche 1 — This Week (money, truth, and the things that page nobody)
- Money path: `tsx-01` (S), `tsx-02` (M), `tsx-08` (S), `tsx-04` (S), `tsx-09` (S), `api-02`/`tsx-03` (S — one change in `mobile-api.ts runCommand`).

- LLM: `llm-01` (S — add `exitPlan` to `BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS` + schema invariant test), `llm-02` (S), `llm-04` (S), `llm-06` (S), `llm-10` (S).

- Security: `sec-01` (S — mirror the `ENCRYPTION_KEY` boot guard), `sec-06` (S), LIVE-20 (S).
- Structure: LIVE-01 root cause (`server-only` on `db.ts`, pure venue helper, dev bundler = prod) (S), `qa-03` (S).
